+<!-- brief extracted from WS-07-service-auth-authz.md -->
+### Task WS-07.3: Service-token permission model, on-behalf-of tokens and outbound header helpers
+**Covers:** MVP-314, MVP-021
+**Severity:** blocker
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-07.1
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** security-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-03 (default assumed: short-lived obo service JWTs, HS256 with AUTH_SECRET_KEY in W1-W3, per-service RS256 keys from W4); D-28 (default assumed: notification-service is the only pager, so safety-service loses `service:write:notification`)
+**Real infra:** no
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `src/solace_security/service_auth.py` (`ServiceIdentity.ANALYTICS = "analytics-service"`; `ServicePermission.ACT_FOR_USER = "service:act_for_user"`, `READ_ONCALL_ROSTER = "service:read:oncall"`, `WRITE_ANALYTICS = "service:write:analytics"`; `SERVICE_PERMISSIONS`: orchestrator gains `ACT_FOR_USER` and `WRITE_PERSONALITY`, safety loses `WRITE_NOTIFICATION`, user-service becomes `[WRITE_NOTIFICATION]`, notification gains `READ_ONCALL_ROSTER`, analytics is `[]`; `OBO_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS = 300`; new `resolve_service_permissions`; `ServiceTokenManager.service_name` property; `create_service_token(..., *, on_behalf_of=None)`; `validate_service_token` checks permissions against the matrix, not the claims; new `correlation_headers`, `service_auth_headers`, `forwarded_auth_headers`)
+- Modify: `src/solace_security/auth.py` (`JWTManager.create_service_token(service_name, permissions=None, expire_minutes=60, *, extra_metadata: dict[str, str] | None = None)` merges `extra_metadata` into `metadata` after `service_name`, which it may not override)
+- Modify: `tests/integration/test_service_integration.py` (only `TestPermissionMatrix::test_user_service_has_no_permissions`: rename to `test_user_service_may_only_send_notifications` and assert `SERVICE_PERMISSIONS[ServiceIdentity.USER] == [ServicePermission.WRITE_NOTIFICATION]`)
+- Test: `tests/solace_security/test_mvp_314_service_token_model.py`
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: `AuthSettings`, `JWTManager` (auth.py); `solace_common.request_context.get_correlation_headers()` (WS-07.1, set per request by WS-04.3's middleware); `structlog.contextvars.get_contextvars()` keys `request_id`, `correlation_id` and `turn_id` (WS-10's `run_turn` re-binds `turn_id` per WebSocket message).
+- Produces (IF-04):
+  - `resolve_service_permissions(service_name: str) -> list[str]`: the values in `SERVICE_PERMISSIONS[ServiceIdentity(service_name)]`; raises `ValueError` for an unknown identity.
+  - `ServiceTokenManager.create_service_token(service_name, target_service=None, additional_permissions=None, expire_minutes=None, *, on_behalf_of: str | None = None) -> ServiceCredentials`. With `on_behalf_of` (must parse as a UUID, else `ValueError`), the token carries `metadata.obo_sub`, lives at most `OBO_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS` (300) and is never written to the cache. Claims stay `sub='service:<name>'`, `type='service'`, `iss='solace-ai'`, `aud='solace-ai-api'`.
+  - `ServiceTokenManager.service_name -> str | None` (from `ServiceAuthSettings.service_name`).
+  - `correlation_headers() -> dict[str, str]`: `get_correlation_headers()` overridden, key by key, by the structlog contextvars `request_id`, `correlation_id` and `turn_id` when bound (the more specific binding wins, so a WebSocket frame's turn id is the one sent).
+  - `async def service_auth_headers(target_service: str, *, on_behalf_of: str | None = None) -> dict[str, str]`: `Authorization: Bearer <token>` from the process singleton plus `correlation_headers()`. Raises `RuntimeError("service auth is not initialized ...")` when `initialize_service_auth()` has not run with a `service_name`, and lets token-minting errors propagate (WS-10's safety client maps a raise to its error path). Plain tokens come from `get_or_create_token`; obo tokens are minted per call.
+  - `forwarded_auth_headers(request: Request) -> dict[str, str]`: the inbound `Authorization` header unchanged plus `correlation_headers()`; used for same-user hops such as therapy to memory (WS-15).
+  - Grants consumed by WS-11 (`ACT_FOR_USER`), WS-13 (`READ_ONCALL_ROSTER`), WS-14 (`WRITE_NOTIFICATION`) and WS-18 (`WRITE_PERSONALITY` for `/detect`, `WRITE_ANALYTICS` held by no service).
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `tests/solace_security/test_mvp_314_service_token_model.py::test_permission_matrix_matches_if04`
+```python
+"""MVP-314 / MVP-021 (supporting): one inter-service credential with receiver-side permissions (IF-04)."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from uuid import uuid4
+
+import jwt
+import pytest
+import structlog
+from starlette.requests import Request
+
+from solace_security import service_auth as sa
+from solace_security.auth import AuthSettings
+from solace_security.service_auth import (
+    SERVICE_PERMISSIONS, ServiceAuthSettings, ServiceIdentity, ServicePermission, ServiceTokenManager,
+    forwarded_auth_headers, initialize_service_auth, resolve_service_permissions, service_auth_headers,
+)
+
+SECRET = "test-secret-key-32-bytes-long!!!"
+
+
+def _manager(name: str) -> ServiceTokenManager:
+    return ServiceTokenManager(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), ServiceAuthSettings(service_name=name))
+
+
+def _claims(token: str) -> dict:
+    return jwt.decode(token, SECRET, algorithms=["HS256"], audience="solace-ai-api", issuer="solace-ai")
+
+
+def _holders(permission) -> set:
+    return {identity for identity, perms in SERVICE_PERMISSIONS.items() if permission in perms}
+
+
+def test_permission_matrix_matches_if04():
+    assert ServicePermission.ACT_FOR_USER.value == "service:act_for_user"
+    assert ServicePermission.READ_ONCALL_ROSTER.value == "service:read:oncall"
+    assert _holders(ServicePermission.ACT_FOR_USER) == {ServiceIdentity.ORCHESTRATOR}
+    assert _holders(ServicePermission.READ_ONCALL_ROSTER) == {ServiceIdentity.NOTIFICATION}
+    assert _holders(ServicePermission.WRITE_NOTIFICATION) == {ServiceIdentity.USER}
+    assert _holders(ServicePermission.WRITE_ANALYTICS) == set()
+    assert ServicePermission.READ_SAFETY in SERVICE_PERMISSIONS[ServiceIdentity.ORCHESTRATOR]
+    assert ServicePermission.WRITE_PERSONALITY in SERVICE_PERMISSIONS[ServiceIdentity.ORCHESTRATOR]
+    assert SERVICE_PERMISSIONS[ServiceIdentity("analytics-service")] == []
+
+
+def test_resolve_service_permissions_comes_from_the_matrix_and_rejects_unknown():
+    assert "service:escalate" not in resolve_service_permissions("memory-service")
+    assert "service:escalate" in resolve_service_permissions("safety-service")
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        resolve_service_permissions("config-service")
+
+
+def test_obo_token_claims_and_ttl():
+    uid = uuid4()
+    creds = _manager("orchestrator-service").create_service_token(
+        "orchestrator-service", "therapy-service", on_behalf_of=str(uid))
+    claims = _claims(creds.token)
+    assert claims["sub"] == "service:orchestrator-service"
+    assert claims["type"] == "service"
+    assert claims["metadata"]["service_name"] == "orchestrator-service"
+    assert claims["metadata"]["obo_sub"] == str(uid)
+    assert claims["exp"] - claims["iat"] <= sa.OBO_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS == 300
+
+
+def test_obo_tokens_are_never_cached():
+    manager = _manager("orchestrator-service")
+    a = manager.create_service_token("orchestrator-service", "memory-service", on_behalf_of=str(uuid4()))
+    b = manager.create_service_token("orchestrator-service", "memory-service", on_behalf_of=str(uuid4()))
+    assert a.token != b.token
+    assert manager.get_cached_token("orchestrator-service", "memory-service") is None
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", ["not-a-uuid", "", "123"])
+def test_obo_requires_a_uuid(bad):
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        _manager("orchestrator-service").create_service_token("orchestrator-service", on_behalf_of=bad)
+
+
+def test_validate_service_token_ignores_claimed_permissions():
+    manager = _manager("orchestrator-service")
+    forged = manager._get_jwt_manager().create_service_token("memory-service", permissions=["service:escalate"])
+    assert manager.validate_service_token(forged, required_permissions=["service:escalate"]).authenticated is False
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_service_auth_headers_carry_identity_and_correlation(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setattr(sa, "_service_token_manager", None)
+    initialize_service_auth(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), ServiceAuthSettings(service_name="notification-service"))
+    structlog.contextvars.bind_contextvars(request_id="r-1", correlation_id="c-1", turn_id="t-1")
+    try:
+        headers = await service_auth_headers("user-service")
+    finally:
+        structlog.contextvars.clear_contextvars()
+        monkeypatch.setattr(sa, "_service_token_manager", None)
+    assert headers["Authorization"].startswith("Bearer ")
+    assert _claims(headers["Authorization"][7:])["sub"] == "service:notification-service"
+    assert (headers["X-Request-ID"], headers["X-Correlation-ID"], headers["X-Turn-ID"]) == ("r-1", "c-1", "t-1")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_request_context_ids_are_used_and_structlog_bindings_win(monkeypatch):
+    from solace_common.request_context import clear_correlation_ids, set_correlation_ids
+    monkeypatch.setattr(sa, "_service_token_manager", None)
+    initialize_service_auth(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), ServiceAuthSettings(service_name="orchestrator-service"))
+    set_correlation_ids(request_id="req-ctx", correlation_id="corr-ctx", turn_id="turn-request")
+    structlog.contextvars.bind_contextvars(turn_id="turn-frame")
+    try:
+        headers = await service_auth_headers("safety-service", on_behalf_of=str(uuid4()))
+    finally:
+        structlog.contextvars.clear_contextvars()
+        clear_correlation_ids()
+        monkeypatch.setattr(sa, "_service_token_manager", None)
+    assert (headers["X-Request-ID"], headers["X-Correlation-ID"], headers["X-Turn-ID"]) == (
+        "req-ctx", "corr-ctx", "turn-frame")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_service_auth_headers_require_an_initialized_identity(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setattr(sa, "_service_token_manager", None)
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="service auth"):
+        await service_auth_headers("user-service")
+
+
+def test_forwarded_auth_headers_copy_the_inbound_authorization():
+    request = Request({"type": "http", "headers": [(b"authorization", b"Bearer inbound-token")]})
+    assert forwarded_auth_headers(request)["Authorization"] == "Bearer inbound-token"
+```
+- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and confirm it fails**
+Run: `$env:PYTHONPATH="D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot;D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot\src"; $env:ENVIRONMENT="test"; & "D:\tmp\mvp-exec\venv\Scripts\python.exe" -m pytest D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot\tests\solace_security\test_mvp_314_service_token_model.py -o "addopts=" -p no:logging -q`

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-07-service-auth-authz.md -->
+### Task WS-07.4: get_current_user accepts orchestrator obo tokens; receivers resolve service permissions
+**Covers:** MVP-314, MVP-021
+**Severity:** blocker
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-07.2, WS-07.3
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** security-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-03 (default assumed: only services granted `service:act_for_user`, today the orchestrator alone, may act for a user; obo principals get `roles=['user']` only)
+**Real infra:** no
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `src/solace_security/middleware.py` (`get_current_user` (lines 230-272): decode once with `decode_token_async(token)` and no expected type; `ACCESS` gives the user as today; `SERVICE` is accepted only when `resolve_service_permissions(<name from sub>)` contains `service:act_for_user`, `metadata.obo_sub` parses as a UUID and `exp - iat <= OBO_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS + clock skew`, yielding `AuthenticatedUser(user_id=obo_sub, token_type=SERVICE, roles=['user'], permissions=[], metadata={'via_service': name}, via_service=name)`; every other type or failure is 401; the RLS user is set to the resolved `user_id`. `get_current_service` (lines 298-332): permissions from `resolve_service_permissions` (unknown identity gives 401), `on_behalf_of` filled and the RLS user set when the token is obo. New `AuthenticatedService.on_behalf_of: str | None = None`. `require_service_permission` (lines 408-444): the inner dependency declares `service: AuthenticatedService = Depends(get_current_service)` so test overrides of `get_current_service` flow through, and it carries the attribute `__solace_service_permissions__ = permissions`. New `find_unguarded_service_routes`.)
+- Test: `tests/solace_security/test_mvp_314_obo_and_receiver_permissions.py`
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: `resolve_service_permissions`, `OBO_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS`, `ServiceTokenManager.create_service_token(..., on_behalf_of=)` (WS-07.3); `JWTManager.create_service_token(..., extra_metadata=)` (WS-07.3); `AuthenticatedUser.via_service` (WS-07.2).
+- Produces (IF-04; public names and signatures unchanged): obo acceptance in `get_current_user`, receiver-side `get_current_service`, `AuthenticatedService.on_behalf_of`, override-friendly `require_service_permission(*permissions: str)`, and `find_unguarded_service_routes(app: FastAPI) -> list[str]`, which returns sorted `"<METHODS> <path>"` strings for every HTTP or WebSocket route whose dependency tree contains `get_current_service` but no dependency carrying `__solace_service_permissions__`. Service lanes (WS-12, WS-13, WS-14, WS-15, WS-16, WS-17, WS-18) call it in their own tests and WS-07.9 runs it over all nine apps.
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `tests/solace_security/test_mvp_314_obo_and_receiver_permissions.py::test_get_current_user_accepts_orchestrator_obo_token`
+```python
+"""MVP-314 / MVP-021 (supporting): obo acceptance and receiver-side service permissions (IF-04)."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import time
+from uuid import uuid4
+
+import jwt
+import pytest
+from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException
+from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials
+from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
+
+from solace_common.request_context import get_current_user_id, reset_current_user_id
+from solace_security import middleware as mw
+from solace_security.auth import AuthSettings, InMemoryTokenBlacklist, JWTManager, TokenType
+from solace_security.middleware import (
+    AuthenticatedService, find_unguarded_service_routes, get_current_service, require_service_permission,
+)
+from solace_security.service_auth import (
+    SERVICE_PERMISSIONS, ServiceAuthSettings, ServiceIdentity, ServicePermission, ServiceTokenManager,
+)
+
+SECRET = "test-secret-key-32-bytes-long!!!"
+
+
+class _URL:
+    path = "/api/v1/x"
+
+
+class _Req:
+    url = _URL()
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def manager(monkeypatch) -> JWTManager:
+    m = JWTManager(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), token_blacklist=InMemoryTokenBlacklist())
+    monkeypatch.setattr(mw, "_get_jwt_manager", lambda: m)
+    return m
+
+
+def _creds(token: str) -> HTTPAuthorizationCredentials:
+    return HTTPAuthorizationCredentials(scheme="Bearer", credentials=token)
+
+
+def _obo(uid=None, service="orchestrator-service") -> str:
+    tm = ServiceTokenManager(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), ServiceAuthSettings(service_name=service))
+    return tm.create_service_token(service, on_behalf_of=str(uid or uuid4())).token
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_get_current_user_accepts_orchestrator_obo_token(manager):
+    reset_current_user_id()
+    uid = uuid4()
+    user = await mw.get_current_user(_Req(), _creds(_obo(uid)))
+    assert user.user_id == str(uid)
+    assert user.via_service == "orchestrator-service"
+    assert user.roles == ["user"]
+    assert user.token_type == TokenType.SERVICE
+    assert get_current_user_id() == str(uid)
+    reset_current_user_id()
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_obo_from_a_service_without_act_for_user_is_rejected(manager):
+    token = manager.create_service_token("therapy-service", extra_metadata={"obo_sub": str(uuid4())})
+    with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as exc:
+        await mw.get_current_user(_Req(), _creds(token))
+    assert exc.value.status_code == 401
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_plain_service_token_and_long_lived_obo_are_rejected(manager):
+    plain = manager.create_service_token("orchestrator-service")
+    long_lived = manager.create_service_token(
+        "orchestrator-service", expire_minutes=60, extra_metadata={"obo_sub": str(uuid4())})
+    for token in (plain, long_lived):
+        with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as exc:
+            await mw.get_current_user(_Req(), _creds(token))
+        assert exc.value.status_code == 401
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_get_current_service_resolves_permissions_receiver_side(manager):
+    forged = manager.create_service_token("memory-service", permissions=["service:escalate"])
+    service = await mw.get_current_service(_Req(), _creds(forged))
+    assert service.service_name == "memory-service"
+    assert sorted(service.permissions) == sorted(p.value for p in SERVICE_PERMISSIONS[ServiceIdentity.MEMORY])
+    assert "service:escalate" not in service.permissions
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_unknown_service_identity_is_rejected(manager):
+    now = int(time.time())
+    token = jwt.encode({"sub": "service:config-service", "type": "service", "jti": "j", "iat": now, "exp": now + 300,
+                        "iss": "solace-ai", "aud": "solace-ai-api", "metadata": {"service_name": "config-service"}},
+                       SECRET, algorithm="HS256")
+    with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as exc:
+        await mw.get_current_service(_Req(), _creds(token))
+    assert exc.value.status_code == 401
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_obo_service_principal_carries_on_behalf_of(manager):
+    uid = uuid4()
+    service = await mw.get_current_service(_Req(), _creds(_obo(uid)))
+    assert service.on_behalf_of == str(uid)
+
+
+def _app() -> FastAPI:
+    app = FastAPI()
+
+    @app.post("/escalate")
+    async def escalate(s: AuthenticatedService = Depends(
+            require_service_permission(ServicePermission.TRIGGER_ESCALATION.value))):
+        return {"service": s.service_name}
+
+    @app.post("/check")
+    async def check(s: AuthenticatedService = Depends(require_service_permission(ServicePermission.READ_SAFETY.value))):
+        return {"service": s.service_name}
+
+    @app.get("/open-status")
+    async def open_status(s: AuthenticatedService = Depends(get_current_service)):
+        return {"ok": True}
+
+    return app
+
+
+def test_claimed_but_ungranted_permission_is_denied(manager):
+    forged = manager.create_service_token("memory-service", permissions=["service:escalate"])
+    response = TestClient(_app()).post("/escalate", headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {forged}"})
+    assert response.status_code == 403
+
+
+def test_require_service_permission_honours_dependency_overrides():
+    app = _app()
+    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_service] = lambda: AuthenticatedService(
+        service_name="orchestrator-service", permissions=[ServicePermission.READ_SAFETY.value])
+    client = TestClient(app)
+    assert client.post("/check").status_code == 200
+    assert client.post("/escalate").status_code == 403
+
+
+def test_find_unguarded_service_routes_lists_only_unguarded_routes():
+    assert find_unguarded_service_routes(_app()) == ["GET /open-status"]
+```

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-07-service-auth-authz.md -->
+### Task WS-07.5: One shared revocation store: Redis in strict environments, URL never logged, no 500 on a Redis error, session revocation
+**Covers:** MVP-186, MVP-188
+**Severity:** medium
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-07.2
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** security-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-26 (default assumed: a limiter-backend failure fails closed on auth routes and open elsewhere, with a metric and an alert; the shared revocation check runs only on non-auth service routes, so it fails open with a metric and a WARNING, while user-service's own identity routes fail closed in WS-08.4; recorded as an owner Ruling); D-14 (strict means staging and production)
+**Real infra:** yes
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `src/solace_security/auth.py` (`TokenBlacklist`: concrete `async def revoke_session(self, session_id: str, until: datetime) -> None` (stores `add(f"sid:{session_id}", until)`), `async def is_session_revoked(self, session_id: str) -> bool` and `def is_session_revoked_sync(self, session_id: str) -> bool`, so subclasses that implement only `add`/`is_blacklisted` keep working; `JWTManager.decode_token_async` checks the jti and, when `payload.session_id` is set, the session; any exception from those lookups is caught once per call, logged as `token_revocation_check_unavailable` (no token, jti or URL) and counted in `solace_token_revocation_check_errors_total`, and the token is treated as not revoked; `decode_token_sync` also checks `is_session_revoked_sync`)
+- Modify: `src/solace_security/middleware.py` (replace `_build_token_blacklist` (lines 123-168) with public `build_token_blacklist`; add `configure_token_blacklist`; `_get_jwt_manager` uses the configured blacklist when one is set; the `url=redis_url` log field (line 153) is removed)
+- Test: `tests/solace_security/test_mvp_186_shared_revocation.py`
+- Test: `tests/realinfra/service-auth-authz/test_mvp_186_cross_service_revocation.py`
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: `TokenPayload.session_id` populated from the `sid` claim (WS-07.2); `solace_infrastructure.runtime.is_strict_environment` (IF-01, WS-04 W0), imported inside the function to avoid an import cycle.
+- Produces (IF-04 blacklist contract):
+  - `build_token_blacklist(redis_client: Any | None = None, *, strict: bool | None = None) -> TokenBlacklist`. Uses the given client (WS-04 passes `runtime.redis`), else a lazy `redis.asyncio.from_url(REDIS_URL, decode_responses=True, socket_timeout=REDIS_TIMEOUT or 1.0)`. With `strict` (default `is_strict_environment()`), a missing `REDIS_URL` with no client, or `AUTH_BLACKLIST_BACKEND=memory`, raises `RuntimeError("REDIS_URL is required for token revocation in strict environments")`. In development/test it falls back to `InMemoryTokenBlacklist` with a WARNING. It never logs the URL.
+  - `configure_token_blacklist(blacklist: TokenBlacklist | None) -> None`: sets (or clears) the process blacklist and clears the `_get_jwt_manager` cache.
+  - Session revocation key: Redis `token_blacklist:sid:<session_id>`, value `"1"`, TTL until the given `until`. WS-08 writes it on logout, password change and reset; every service reads it through `get_current_user`.
+  - Metric `solace_token_revocation_check_errors_total` (counter, no labels; the scrape target names the service).
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** in `tests/solace_security/test_mvp_186_shared_revocation.py::test_strict_environment_without_redis_url_fails_loud` and the real-infra file
+```python
+"""MVP-186 / MVP-188: one shared revocation store (Redis in strict), URL never logged, errors never 500."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import time
+from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
+from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+
+import jwt
+import pytest
+import structlog
+from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials
+from prometheus_client import REGISTRY
+
+from solace_security import middleware as mw
+from solace_security.auth import AuthSettings, InMemoryTokenBlacklist, JWTManager, RedisTokenBlacklist
+
+SECRET = "test-secret-key-32-bytes-long!!!"
+SID = "0b6f8a52-1111-4000-8000-000000000001"
+
+
+class _URL:
+    path = "/api/v1/x"
+
+
+class _Req:
+    url = _URL()
+
+
+def _access(sid=SID, jti="j-1") -> str:
+    now = int(time.time())
+    return jwt.encode({"sub": "u-1", "type": "access", "jti": jti, "sid": sid, "iat": now, "exp": now + 600,
+                       "iss": "solace-ai", "aud": "solace-ai-api"}, SECRET, algorithm="HS256")
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _isolated_manager(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("AUTH_SECRET_KEY", SECRET)
+    monkeypatch.delenv("AUTH_BLACKLIST_BACKEND", raising=False)
+    mw._get_jwt_manager.cache_clear()
+    yield
+    mw.configure_token_blacklist(None)
+    mw._get_jwt_manager.cache_clear()
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("env", ["staging", "production"])
+def test_strict_environment_without_redis_url_fails_loud(monkeypatch, env):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", env)
+    monkeypatch.delenv("REDIS_URL", raising=False)
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="REDIS_URL"):
+        mw._get_jwt_manager()
+
+
+def test_memory_override_is_refused_in_strict(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", "production")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://:pw-override@redis:6379/0")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("AUTH_BLACKLIST_BACKEND", "memory")
+    with patch("redis.asyncio.from_url", return_value=MagicMock()), pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
+        mw.build_token_blacklist()
+
+
+def test_redis_url_is_never_logged(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://:s3cr3t-pw-188@redis:6379/0")
+    with patch("redis.asyncio.from_url", return_value=MagicMock()), structlog.testing.capture_logs() as logs:
+        blacklist = mw.build_token_blacklist()
+    assert isinstance(blacklist, RedisTokenBlacklist)
+    assert "s3cr3t-pw-188" not in repr(logs)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_revoking_a_session_rejects_every_token_of_that_session_only():
+    blacklist = InMemoryTokenBlacklist()
+    manager = JWTManager(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), token_blacklist=blacklist)
+    await blacklist.revoke_session(SID, datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=15))
+    for token in (_access(jti="a"), _access(jti="b")):
+        result = await manager.validate_access_token_async(token)
+        assert (result.success, result.error_code) == (False, "TOKEN_REVOKED")
+    other = await manager.validate_access_token_async(_access(sid="0b6f8a52-2222-4000-8000-000000000002", jti="c"))
+    assert other.success is True
+
+
+class _DownRedis:
+    async def get(self, key):
+        raise ConnectionError("redis down")
+
+    async def set(self, *args, **kwargs):
+        raise ConnectionError("redis down")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_revocation_backend_error_fails_open_with_one_metric_increment(monkeypatch):
+    manager = JWTManager(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), token_blacklist=RedisTokenBlacklist(_DownRedis()))
+    monkeypatch.setattr(mw, "_get_jwt_manager", lambda: manager)
+    before = REGISTRY.get_sample_value("solace_token_revocation_check_errors_total") or 0.0
+    creds = HTTPAuthorizationCredentials(scheme="Bearer", credentials=_access())
+    user = await mw.get_current_user(_Req(), creds)
+    assert user.user_id == "u-1"
+    assert REGISTRY.get_sample_value("solace_token_revocation_check_errors_total") == before + 1
+
+
+def test_configured_blacklist_is_used_by_the_middleware():
+    shared = InMemoryTokenBlacklist()
+    mw.configure_token_blacklist(shared)
+    assert mw._get_jwt_manager()._blacklist is shared
+```
+```python
+"""tests/realinfra/service-auth-authz/test_mvp_186_cross_service_revocation.py
+MVP-186 on real Redis: a revocation written by one service is seen by every other; pins the sid key."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import time
+from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
+
+import jwt
+import pytest
+import redis.asyncio as aioredis
+
+from solace_security.auth import AuthSettings, JWTManager
+from solace_security.middleware import build_token_blacklist
+
+pytestmark = [pytest.mark.realinfra, pytest.mark.integration]
+SECRET = "test-secret-key-32-bytes-long!!!"
+SID = "7d2c9e10-3333-4000-8000-000000000003"
+
+
+def _token(jti: str) -> str:
+    now = int(time.time())
+    return jwt.encode({"sub": "u-1", "type": "access", "jti": jti, "sid": SID, "iat": now, "exp": now + 600,
+                       "iss": "solace-ai", "aud": "solace-ai-api"}, SECRET, algorithm="HS256")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_revocation_propagates_between_two_service_processes(redis_url):
+    a_client = aioredis.from_url(redis_url, decode_responses=True)
+    b_client = aioredis.from_url(redis_url, decode_responses=True)
+    try:
+        service_a = JWTManager(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), token_blacklist=build_token_blacklist(a_client))
+        service_b = JWTManager(AuthSettings(secret_key=SECRET), token_blacklist=build_token_blacklist(b_client))
+        assert (await service_b.validate_access_token_async(_token("j-1"))).success is True
+        await service_a.revoke_token(_token("j-1"))
+        assert (await service_b.validate_access_token_async(_token("j-1"))).error_code == "TOKEN_REVOKED"
+        await service_a._blacklist.revoke_session(SID, datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=15))
+        assert (await service_b.validate_access_token_async(_token("j-2"))).error_code == "TOKEN_REVOKED"
+        assert await b_client.get(f"token_blacklist:sid:{SID}") == "1"
+        assert 0 < await b_client.ttl(f"token_blacklist:sid:{SID}") <= 15 * 60
+    finally:
+        await a_client.aclose()
+        await b_client.aclose()
+```

+### Task WS-07.6: Bounded, self-sweeping in-memory rate-limit store
+**Covers:** MVP-311
+**Severity:** low
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** none
+**Implementer model:** sonnet
+**Review:** security-critical
+**Decision gate:** none
+**Real infra:** no
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `infrastructure/api_gateway/rate_limiting.py` (`RateLimitStore.__init__(self, max_entries: int = DEFAULT_MAX_ENTRIES, sweep_every: int = 1000)` with class constant `DEFAULT_MAX_ENTRIES = 100_000`; `_counters` becomes an `OrderedDict` with move-to-end on every touch and least-recently-used eviction once `max_entries` is exceeded; `increment` calls `cleanup_expired()` every `sweep_every` increments; the unused `_expiry` dict (line 128) is removed; `RedisRateLimitStore._sync_fallback` uses the bounded default)
+- Test: `infrastructure/api_gateway/tests/test_mvp_311_rate_limit_store_bounds.py`
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: none.
+- Produces: `RateLimitStore(max_entries: int = 100_000, sweep_every: int = 1000)`; behaviour of `increment`, `get_count`, `reset` and `cleanup_expired` is otherwise unchanged. WS-07.7 builds dev/test limiters on it.
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `infrastructure/api_gateway/tests/test_mvp_311_rate_limit_store_bounds.py::test_expired_counters_are_swept_during_normal_increments`
+```python
+"""MVP-311: the in-memory rate-limit store must not grow without bound."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import infrastructure.api_gateway.rate_limiting as rl
+
+
+def _policy(limit: int = 5) -> rl.RateLimitPolicy:
+    return rl.RateLimitPolicy(name="bounded", limit=limit, window=rl.RateLimitWindow.MINUTE)
+
+
+def test_expired_counters_are_swept_during_normal_increments(monkeypatch):
+    clock = {"t": 1000.0}
+    monkeypatch.setattr(rl.time, "time", lambda: clock["t"])
+    store = rl.RateLimitStore(sweep_every=100)
+    for i in range(5000):
+        store.increment(_policy(), f"ip:{i}")
+    clock["t"] += 3 * 60
+    for i in range(100):
+        store.increment(_policy(), f"fresh:{i}")
+    assert len(store._counters) <= 100
+
+
+def test_store_is_bounded_and_keeps_the_most_recent_keys():
+    store = rl.RateLimitStore(max_entries=100)
+    for i in range(500):
+        store.increment(_policy(), f"ip:{i}")
+    assert len(store._counters) <= 100
+    assert store.get_count(_policy(), "ip:499") == 1
+    assert store.get_count(_policy(), "ip:0") == 0
+
+
+def test_a_hot_key_survives_eviction_pressure():
+    store = rl.RateLimitStore(max_entries=50)
+    for i in range(300):
+        store.increment(_policy(limit=1000), "attacker")
+        store.increment(_policy(limit=1000), f"noise:{i}")
+    assert store.get_count(_policy(limit=1000), "attacker") == 300
+
+
+def test_redis_store_sync_fallback_is_bounded():
+    assert rl.RedisRateLimitStore(object())._sync_fallback._max_entries == rl.RateLimitStore.DEFAULT_MAX_ENTRIES
+```

+Expected: FAIL with `TypeError: RateLimitStore.__init__() got an unexpected keyword argument 'sweep_every'` (and `'max_entries'`): the store has no bound and `cleanup_expired()` has no caller, so 5,000 callers leave 5,000 counters.

Expected: all pass

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-07-service-auth-authz.md -->
+### Task WS-07.7: install_rate_limiting: real-client keying behind Caddy, auth-route policies, Redis backend and the D-26 failure policy
+**Covers:** MVP-101, MVP-185
+**Severity:** high
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-07.6
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** security-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-26 (default assumed: the app-level Redis limiter keyed on user id or the real client IP with TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS=1 is the control; on a backend failure auth routes return 503 and other routes pass, with a metric and an alert)
+**Real infra:** yes
+**From the W0 bug round:** C-2: strict boot and the rate limiter. In strict, user-service's limiter keys on the socket peer (hops 0), so all anonymous users behind Caddy share one IP bucket; `TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS` is set in compose but nothing reads it (orchestrator reads `ORCHESTRATOR_TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS`; WS-03.5 review W035-SEC-1 context). Add a RED case: behind one trusted hop, two different client IPs get separate buckets, and a spoofed `X-Forwarded-For` beyond the trusted hops is ignored. (The bug-round hunter text was not preserved; this line is rebuilt from the 2026-11-03 ledger summary and this task's own scope. Re-confirm the failure in a RED test before fixing.)
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `src/solace_security/rate_limit.py` (new `resolve_trusted_proxy_hops`, `classify_route`, `AUTH_ROUTE_LIMITS`, `build_rate_limiter`, `install_rate_limiting`; `client_ip(request, trusted_proxy_hops: int | None = None)` where `None` means `resolve_trusted_proxy_hops()`; `RateLimitMiddleware` gains `auth_route_predicate: Callable[[str], bool] | None = None`, catches any limiter exception, increments `solace_rate_limit_backend_errors_total{service}`, logs `rate_limit_backend_unavailable` without the identifier, and returns 503 `{"error": {"code": "RATE_LIMIT_UNAVAILABLE", "message": "Authentication is temporarily unavailable. Please retry shortly."}}` with `Retry-After: 5` on auth routes or passes the request through elsewhere)
+- Modify: `infrastructure/api_gateway/rate_limiting.py` (`create_solace_rate_limiter`: the Redis requirement (lines 367-374) uses `is_strict_environment()` instead of `env == "production"`)
+- Test: `tests/solace_security/test_mvp_101_install_rate_limiting.py`
+- Test: `tests/realinfra/service-auth-authz/test_mvp_101_shared_redis_limits.py`
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: `RateLimitStore` (WS-07.6); `create_solace_rate_limiter`, `RateLimitPolicy`; `_get_jwt_manager().validate_access_token` (signature-checked keying only; the route still authenticates); `solace_infrastructure.runtime.is_strict_environment` and a `ServiceRuntimeSpec`-shaped `spec` with `service_name` and `environment` (IF-01, WS-04 W0), imported lazily.
+- Produces (IF-04 rate limiting):
+  - `resolve_trusted_proxy_hops(environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> int`: `TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS` when set (an int >= 0), else 1 in strict environments and 0 otherwise.
+  - `classify_route(path: str) -> tuple[str | None, bool]`: route name and whether it is an auth route. Auth routes are `/api/v1/auth/*` and `/api/v1/users/me/password`. Named routes and per-minute limits (`AUTH_ROUTE_LIMITS`): `auth-login` 20, `auth-register` 10, `auth-refresh` 30, `auth-resend` 5, `auth-verify` 20, `auth-forgot` 5, `auth-reset` 10, `auth-reauth` 10, `auth-mfa` 10 (`/mfa/verify`, `/mfa/enroll/*`), `auth-oauth` 30 (`/oauth/google/*`), `auth-session` 30 (`/logout`, `/revoke-token`), `auth-password-change` 5; `/api/v1/orchestrator/chat` maps to the existing `chat-message` policy and is not an auth route.
+  - `build_rate_limiter(service_name: str, *, redis_client: Any | None) -> RateLimiter`: `create_solace_rate_limiter` plus the route policies, with limits overridden by the optional JSON object `RATE_LIMIT_OVERRIDES_JSON` (route name to per-minute limit).
+  - `install_rate_limiting(app: FastAPI, spec: ServiceRuntimeSpec, *, force: bool = False, redis_client: Any | None = None) -> None`: installs `RateLimitMiddleware` keyed `user:<sub>` for a signature-valid bearer, else `ip:<client_ip>`; Redis from `redis_client`, else a lazy `redis.asyncio.from_url(REDIS_URL, socket_timeout=0.5, socket_connect_timeout=0.5)`; strict with neither raises `RuntimeError("REDIS_URL is required for rate limiting in strict environments")`; development/test without Redis uses the bounded in-memory store with a WARNING. Unless `force`, it does nothing when the environment (spec or `ENVIRONMENT`) starts with `test` and is not strict, so suites that share a module-level app do not bleed counters. Call it before CORS is added so CORS stays outermost.
+  - Metric `solace_rate_limit_backend_errors_total{service}`.
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** in `tests/solace_security/test_mvp_101_install_rate_limiting.py::test_login_bucket_is_per_real_client_not_per_proxy` and the real-infra file
+```python
+"""MVP-101 / MVP-185: limiter keyed on the real client behind Caddy, Redis-backed, D-26 failure policy."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+
+import pytest
+from fastapi import FastAPI
+from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
+from prometheus_client import REGISTRY
+
+from solace_security.rate_limit import classify_route, install_rate_limiting, resolve_trusted_proxy_hops
+
+LOGIN = "/api/v1/auth/login"
+
+
+def _spec(env: str = "development"):
+    return SimpleNamespace(service_name="user-service", environment=env, rate_limited=True, edge=True)
+
+
+def _app(spec, **kwargs) -> FastAPI:
+    app = FastAPI()
+
+    @app.post(LOGIN)
+    async def login():
+        return {"ok": False}
+
+    @app.get("/api/v1/users/me")
+    async def me():
+        return {"ok": True}
+
+    @app.get("/live")
+    async def live():
+        return {"status": "alive"}
+
+    install_rate_limiting(app, spec, force=True, **kwargs)
+    return app
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _env(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("REDIS_URL", raising=False)
+    monkeypatch.delenv("RATE_LIMIT_OVERRIDES_JSON", raising=False)
+    monkeypatch.setenv("TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS", "1")
+
+
+def test_login_bucket_is_per_real_client_not_per_proxy():
+    with TestClient(_app(_spec())) as client:
+        attacker = {"x-forwarded-for": "198.51.100.7"}
+        assert [client.post(LOGIN, headers=attacker).status_code for _ in range(20)] == [200] * 20
+        assert client.post(LOGIN, headers=attacker).status_code == 429
+        assert client.post(LOGIN, headers={"x-forwarded-for": "203.0.113.9"}).status_code == 200
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("environ,expected", [
+    ({"ENVIRONMENT": "production"}, 1), ({"ENVIRONMENT": "staging"}, 1),
+    ({"ENVIRONMENT": "development"}, 0), ({"ENVIRONMENT": "development", "TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS": "2"}, 2)])
+def test_trusted_proxy_hops_resolution(environ, expected):
+    assert resolve_trusted_proxy_hops(environ) == expected
+
+
+def test_auth_routes_are_classified():
+    assert classify_route(LOGIN) == ("auth-login", True)
+    assert classify_route("/api/v1/auth/register") == ("auth-register", True)
+    assert classify_route("/api/v1/auth/oauth/google/exchange") == ("auth-oauth", True)
+    assert classify_route("/api/v1/users/me/password") == ("auth-password-change", True)
+    assert classify_route("/api/v1/orchestrator/chat") == ("chat-message", False)
+    assert classify_route("/api/v1/users/me") == (None, False)
+
+
+class _DownRedis:
+    async def eval(self, *args, **kwargs):
+        raise ConnectionError("redis down")
+
+    async def ttl(self, *args, **kwargs):
+        raise ConnectionError("redis down")
+
+
+def test_backend_failure_fails_closed_on_auth_routes_and_open_elsewhere():
+    labels = {"service": "user-service"}
+    before = REGISTRY.get_sample_value("solace_rate_limit_backend_errors_total", labels) or 0.0
+    with TestClient(_app(_spec(), redis_client=_DownRedis())) as client:
+        denied = client.post(LOGIN)
+        assert denied.status_code == 503
+        assert denied.json()["error"]["code"] == "RATE_LIMIT_UNAVAILABLE"
+        assert denied.headers["Retry-After"] == "5"
+        assert client.get("/api/v1/users/me").status_code == 200
+    assert REGISTRY.get_sample_value("solace_rate_limit_backend_errors_total", labels) == before + 2
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("env", ["staging", "production"])
+def test_strict_environment_requires_redis(env):
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="REDIS_URL"):
+        _app(_spec(env))
+
+
+def test_named_limits_can_be_overridden(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("RATE_LIMIT_OVERRIDES_JSON", '{"auth-login": 2}')
+    with TestClient(_app(_spec())) as client:
+        headers = {"x-forwarded-for": "192.0.2.1"}
+        assert [client.post(LOGIN, headers=headers).status_code for _ in range(3)] == [200, 200, 429]
+
+
+def test_liveness_probe_is_exempt():
+    with TestClient(_app(_spec())) as client:
+        assert all(client.get("/live").status_code == 200 for _ in range(50))
+```
+```python
+"""tests/realinfra/service-auth-authz/test_mvp_101_shared_redis_limits.py
+MVP-101 on real Redis: two instances of a service share one login bucket per real client."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import random
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+
+import pytest
+from fastapi import FastAPI
+from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
+
+from solace_security.rate_limit import install_rate_limiting
+
+pytestmark = [pytest.mark.realinfra, pytest.mark.integration]
+LOGIN = "/api/v1/auth/login"
+
+
+def _instance() -> FastAPI:
+    app = FastAPI()
+
+    @app.post(LOGIN)
+    async def login():
+        return {"ok": False}
+
+    install_rate_limiting(app, SimpleNamespace(service_name="user-service", environment="development",
+                                               rate_limited=True, edge=True), force=True)
+    return app
+
+
+def test_two_instances_share_one_login_bucket(redis_url, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("REDIS_URL", redis_url)
+    monkeypatch.setenv("TRUSTED_PROXY_HOPS", "1")
+    headers = {"x-forwarded-for": f"198.51.100.{random.randint(1, 250)}"}
+    with TestClient(_instance()) as a, TestClient(_instance()) as b:
+        codes = [a.post(LOGIN, headers=headers).status_code for _ in range(10)]
+        codes += [b.post(LOGIN, headers=headers).status_code for _ in range(11)]
+    assert codes[:20] == [200] * 20
+    assert codes[20] == 429
+```

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-09-phi-codec-audit-llm.md -->
+### Task WS-09.18: Bedrock Converse provider, the only real-user LLM route in staging and production
+
+**Covers:** none (implements D-04 as decided 2026-10-19; supports MVP-057 and MVP-199)
+**Severity:** high
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-09.3, WS-01.2
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** security-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-04 (decided 2026-10-19, changed: real-user LLM traffic goes to Anthropic models on AWS Bedrock under the AWS BAA; strict environments require `LLM_PROVIDER=bedrock`; `LLM_PROVIDER=portkey` is allowed only in development and test with synthetic data; the LLM stays off for real users until the owner accepts the BAA in AWS Artifact and it is recorded through `LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED` (WS-09.4); unchanged: caching off, no semantic cache, minimum-necessary prompts, tests use only stubs and fakes)
+**Real infra:** no
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `services/shared/infrastructure/llm_client.py` (`LLMClientSettings.provider: Literal["bedrock", "portkey"] = "portkey"`, read as `LLM_PROVIDER`; new `class BedrockSettings(BaseSettings)` with `model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_prefix="BEDROCK_", extra="ignore")` (process environment only, no `.env` file) and fields `region: str = "us-east-1"`, `model_id: str = ""`, `endpoint_url: str = ""`, `timeout_seconds: float = Field(default=20.0, gt=0)`; `UnifiedLLMClient.__init__(self, settings=None, *, client_factory=None, bedrock_settings=None, bedrock_client_factory=None)`; module-level `_default_bedrock_client_factory(*, region_name, endpoint_url, config)` that imports boto3 lazily and returns `boto3.session.Session().client("bedrock-runtime", region_name=region_name, endpoint_url=endpoint_url, config=config)`; private `_bedrock_config(settings: BedrockSettings) -> botocore.config.Config`; pure helpers `_to_converse_request(messages, *, model_id, max_tokens, temperature) -> dict[str, Any]` and `_converse_text(response: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str`; the routing and strict refusals in `initialize()`; the Bedrock branch of `_execute_completion`; the botocore error mapping in `generate()`; boto3 and botocore are imported only on the Bedrock path, so the module still imports where they are absent)
+- Create: `tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_d04_bedrock_provider.py`
+- Test: `tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_d04_bedrock_provider.py`
+
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: WS-09.2 `UnifiedLLMClient(..., client_factory=...)`, `LLMConfigurationError` and the local `_strict_environment()`; WS-09.3 `LLMUnavailableError`, `LLMResult`, `generate()` and `generate_with_fallback()`; WS-01.2's lock pins of `boto3` and `botocore` (CR-16; until the venv is re-synced to the lock, its system site packages provide boto3 and botocore 1.40.61).
+- Produces (IF-15 as amended by D-04; the setting names are the decision's IF-02 names, registered by WS-03 through CR-14):
+  - `LLM_PROVIDER` in {bedrock, portkey} (`LLMClientSettings.provider`; default `portkey` for development and test, so a strict deployment that omits it fails at startup). `BedrockSettings` reads `BEDROCK_REGION` (default `us-east-1`), `BEDROCK_MODEL_ID` (no default), `BEDROCK_ENDPOINT_URL` (test and E2E only) and `BEDROCK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` (default 20). `UnifiedLLMClient(settings=None, *, client_factory=None, bedrock_settings=None, bedrock_client_factory=None)`; `bedrock_settings` defaults to `BedrockSettings()`, so existing `UnifiedLLMClient(settings)` call sites need no change.
+  - `bedrock_client_factory(*, region_name, endpoint_url, config)` receives exactly these three keywords and never credentials (boto3's default chain resolves the host's instance role, or `AWS_PROFILE` in local development) and returns an object with `converse(**request) -> dict`. `config` is `botocore.config.Config(connect_timeout=5, read_timeout=BEDROCK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, retries={"total_max_attempts": 2, "mode": "standard"})`, plus `signature_version=botocore.UNSIGNED` only when `BEDROCK_ENDPOINT_URL` is set, so the fake LLM needs no AWS credentials; `endpoint_url` is `None` when the setting is empty.
+  - `initialize()`: in strict environments `LLM_PROVIDER=portkey` raises `LLMConfigurationError` naming `LLM_PROVIDER`, and a non-empty `BEDROCK_ENDPOINT_URL` raises `LLMConfigurationError` naming the variable and never its value; both checks run first, before any factory call and whatever the approval flag says. With `LLM_PROVIDER=bedrock`, an empty `BEDROCK_MODEL_ID` builds no client and leaves `is_available=False` (ERROR `llm_disabled_missing_configuration` with `missing=["BEDROCK_MODEL_ID"]` in strict, WARNING outside); WS-09.4 makes it a startup error in strict once `LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED=true`, the only state in which the model would be called. boto3 or botocore not importable, or a factory exception, raises `LLMConfigurationError` in strict and disables outside (WS-09.2's rule; only the exception type name is logged).
+  - Request: `_to_converse_request` puts every system-role message, in order, into `system=[{"text": ...}]`; maps `assistant` to assistant and every other role to user; drops empty texts and any assistant turn before the first user turn; merges consecutive same-role turns into one message with several text blocks; sends `inferenceConfig={"maxTokens": ..., "temperature": ...}` and never `topP`, so a model that refuses both sampling parameters together cannot fail every task-preset call (presets keep only their temperature on this route); never sends `cachePoint`, `requestMetadata`, `additionalModelRequestFields`, `promptVariables` or `guardrailConfig`, so no prompt, user id or trace metadata reaches a cache or AWS-side metadata. The call is `await asyncio.wait_for(asyncio.to_thread(client.converse, **request), timeout=BEDROCK_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)`.
+  - Reply: `_converse_text` joins the `text` blocks of `output.message.content`; `generate_with_fallback` returns `LLMResult(text=..., degraded=False, provider="bedrock")`.
+  - Teardown: the Bedrock client is held where WS-09.2's `shutdown()` finds it, so `shutdown()` also calls the botocore `bedrock-runtime` client's synchronous `close()` and never raises (WS-04's W1 row, for WS-04.4's ordered teardown; pinned by `test_d04_shutdown_closes_the_bedrock_client`).
+  - Errors, mapped to the existing IF-15 types only: `asyncio.TimeoutError`, `botocore.exceptions.ReadTimeoutError` and `botocore.exceptions.ConnectTimeoutError` become `LLMUnavailableError(reason="timeout")`; `botocore.exceptions.ClientError` and any other `BotoCoreError` become `reason="provider_error"`, raised `from None`; a reply without text becomes `reason="empty_response"`. The `llm_generation_failed` line carries `reason`, `service`, `error_type` and, for a `ClientError`, `error_code` (for example `ThrottlingException`); no log line, error message or cache ever holds prompt or reply text.
+  - Consumers: WS-09.4 (the egress gate and `readiness()` over both providers), WS-09.5 (caching refused with `LLM_PROVIDER=bedrock`), WS-09.6 (the message order this converter hoists and merges); every service lane that builds a `UnifiedLLMClient` (the CR-7 set: WS-10, WS-11, WS-12, WS-15, WS-16, WS-18), with no call-site change; WS-05.1's fake LLM `POST /model/{model_id}/converse` route and the E2E stacks (CR-3, WS-05.21, which depends on this task); WS-03's env registry (CR-14) and the instance-role grant (CR-15); WS-01.2's lock pins (CR-16). It also answers WS-03's W1 row for WS-03.19 and WS-03.20 (exactly the five WS-03.19 names, credentials only from the default boto3 chain, never `AWS_*` key variables, and the strict refusals of `BEDROCK_ENDPOINT_URL` and `LLM_PROVIDER=portkey`; WS-03.19 depends on this task) and WS-04's W1 row for WS-04.4 (the teardown above).
+

+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import sys
+import threading
+

+        "LLM_PROVIDER", "LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED", "LLM_ENABLE_CACHING",

+    ):

+    monkeypatch.setenv("AWS_CONFIG_FILE", str(tmp_path / "no-config"))

+
+def _stubbed():

+    return runtime, Stubber(runtime)
+
+
+def _client(runtime=None, calls=None, **bedrock):
+    def factory(**kwargs):
+        if calls is not None:
+            calls.append(kwargs)
+        return runtime if runtime is not None else object()
+
+    return UnifiedLLMClient(
+        LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None, provider="bedrock"),
+        bedrock_settings=BedrockSettings(**{"model_id": MODEL_ID, **bedrock}),
+        bedrock_client_factory=factory,
+    )
+
+
+class _RecordingLogger:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.events = []
+
+    def _record(self, event, **kwargs):
+        self.events.append((event, kwargs))
+
+    debug = info = warning = error = exception = _record
+
+
+class _HangingRuntime:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.release = threading.Event()
+
+    def converse(self, **_):
+        self.release.wait(5)
+        return REPLY
+
+
+class _TimeoutRuntime:
+    def converse(self, **_):
+        raise ReadTimeoutError(endpoint_url="http://fake-llm.invalid")
+
+
+async def test_d04_converse_request_shape_and_reply():
+    runtime, stubber = _stubbed()
+    stubber.add_response("converse", REPLY, {
+        "modelId": MODEL_ID,
+        "system": [{"text": "SYS"}, {"text": "[User Context] memories"}],
+        "messages": [
+            {"role": "user", "content": [{"text": "a"}]},
+            {"role": "assistant", "content": [{"text": "ok"}]},
+            {"role": "user", "content": [{"text": "b"}, {"text": "now"}]},
+        ],
+        "inferenceConfig": {"maxTokens": 1024, "temperature": 0.2},
+    })
+    client = _client(runtime)
+    await client.initialize()
+    with stubber:

+        stubber.assert_no_pending_responses()
+    assert result == LLMResult(text="Synthetic reply.", degraded=False, provider="bedrock")
+
+
+async def test_d04_identical_prompts_are_never_served_from_a_cache():
+    runtime, stubber = _stubbed()
+    expected = {

+        "inferenceConfig": {"maxTokens": 1024, "temperature": 0.7},
+    }
+    stubber.add_response("converse", REPLY, expected)
+    stubber.add_response("converse", REPLY, expected)

+        stubber.assert_no_pending_responses()

+
+async def test_d04_client_error_is_provider_error_without_prompt_text(monkeypatch):
+    recorder = _RecordingLogger()
+    monkeypatch.setattr(llm_module, "logger", recorder)
+    runtime, stubber = _stubbed()

+

+class _ClosableRuntime:
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.closed = False
+
+    def close(self):  # botocore's BaseClient.close() is synchronous

+async def test_d04_shutdown_closes_the_bedrock_client():
+    runtime = _ClosableRuntime()
+    client = _client(runtime)

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-09-phi-codec-audit-llm.md -->
+### Task WS-09.3: generate() raises LLMUnavailableError and the fallback path returns LLMResult
+
+**Covers:** MVP-139
+**Severity:** medium
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-09.2
+**Implementer model:** sonnet
+**Review:** standard
+**Decision gate:** none
+**Real infra:** no
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `services/shared/infrastructure/llm_client.py` (new `class LLMUnavailableError(Exception)` with attributes `reason: str` and `service: str`; new `@dataclass(frozen=True) class LLMResult: text: str; degraded: bool; provider: str`; `generate()` raises instead of returning `"(LLM unavailable)"` or `""`; `generate_with_fallback(...) -> LLMResult`)
+- Modify: `services/shared/infrastructure/__init__.py` and `services/shared/__init__.py` (export `LLMUnavailableError`, `LLMResult`)
+- Test: `tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_139_llm_unavailable.py` (create)
+
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: `UnifiedLLMClient(..., client_factory=...)` from WS-09.2.
+- Produces (IF-15): `LLMUnavailableError.reason` in {"not_configured", "disabled", "timeout", "provider_error", "empty_response"}; `str(error) == f"LLM unavailable ({reason})"` and never contains prompt, response or provider error text; `generate_with_fallback(system_prompt, user_message, fallback_response, **kwargs) -> LLMResult` returns `LLMResult(text=fallback_response, degraded=True, provider="fallback")` on `LLMUnavailableError` and `LLMResult(text=<reply>, degraded=False, provider=settings.primary_provider)` otherwise. `_error_count` increments for timeout, provider_error and empty_response; the log line `llm_generation_failed` carries `reason`, `service` and `error_type` (exception class name) only.
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_139_llm_unavailable.py`
+```python
+"""MVP-139: generate() raises LLMUnavailableError; generate_with_fallback returns LLMResult."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncio
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import SecretStr
+
+from services.shared.infrastructure.llm_client import LLMClientSettings, UnifiedLLMClient
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _env(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", "test")
+
+
+def _client(create=None, *, key="pk-test", timeout=1):
+    fake = SimpleNamespace(chat=SimpleNamespace(completions=SimpleNamespace(create=create)))
+    settings = LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None, portkey_api_key=SecretStr(key), timeout_seconds=timeout)
+    return UnifiedLLMClient(settings, client_factory=lambda **_: fake)
+
+
+def _reply(text):
+    return SimpleNamespace(choices=[SimpleNamespace(message=SimpleNamespace(content=text))])
+
+
+async def test_mvp_139_unavailable_client_never_returns_a_sentinel():
+    client = _client(key="")
+    await client.initialize()
+    with pytest.raises(Exception) as exc:
+        await client.generate(system_prompt="s", user_message="hello", service_name="t")
+    assert type(exc.value).__name__ == "LLMUnavailableError"
+    assert exc.value.reason == "not_configured"
+
+
+async def test_mvp_139_provider_error_raises_without_echoing_text():
+    from services.shared.infrastructure.llm_client import LLMUnavailableError
+
+    async def boom(**_):
+        raise RuntimeError("upstream 500: prompt was 'hello there'")
+
+    client = _client(boom)
+    await client.initialize()
+    with pytest.raises(LLMUnavailableError) as exc:
+        await client.generate(system_prompt="s", user_message="hello there", service_name="t")
+    assert exc.value.reason == "provider_error"
+    assert "hello" not in str(exc.value)
+    assert client.get_statistics()["total_errors"] == 1
+
+
+async def test_mvp_139_timeout_raises_with_reason_timeout():
+    from services.shared.infrastructure.llm_client import LLMUnavailableError
+
+    async def hang(**_):
+        await asyncio.sleep(30)
+
+    client = _client(hang)
+    await client.initialize()
+    with pytest.raises(LLMUnavailableError) as exc:
+        await client.generate(system_prompt="s", user_message="u", service_name="t")
+    assert exc.value.reason == "timeout"
+
+
+async def test_mvp_139_empty_completion_raises():
+    from services.shared.infrastructure.llm_client import LLMUnavailableError
+
+    async def empty(**_):
+        return SimpleNamespace(choices=[])
+
+    client = _client(empty)
+    await client.initialize()
+    with pytest.raises(LLMUnavailableError) as exc:

+async def test_mvp_139_fallback_success_is_not_degraded():

+

+    await client.initialize()
+    result = await client.generate_with_fallback(
+        system_prompt="s", user_message="u", fallback_response="template", service_name="t"
+    )

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-09-phi-codec-audit-llm.md -->
+### Task WS-09.4: LLM settings read the documented keys and honour the PHI-egress decision
+
+**Covers:** MVP-057
+**Severity:** high
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-09.18
+**Implementer model:** sonnet
+**Review:** standard (Opus reviewer per the WS-09 model policy)
+**Decision gate:** D-04 (decided 2026-10-19, changed: strict environments run only `LLM_PROVIDER=bedrock` (WS-09.18) and the BAA that matters is the AWS BAA, accepted by the owner in AWS Artifact; unchanged: in strict environments `LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED=false` disables the real provider until that BAA is accepted and recorded; approved plus missing configuration, here `BEDROCK_MODEL_ID`, is a startup error)
+**Real infra:** no
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `services/shared/infrastructure/llm_client.py` (`LLMClientSettings.phi_egress_approved: bool = False`, read as `LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED`; `initialize()` strict gating for both providers; new `async def readiness(self) -> tuple[bool, str]`; `_strict_environment()` now delegates to `solace_infrastructure.runtime.is_strict_environment` through a function-local import)
+- Test: `tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_057_llm_settings_egress.py` (create)
+
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: `is_strict_environment()` (IF-01, WS-04); WS-09.18 `LLMClientSettings.provider`, `BedrockSettings`, `bedrock_client_factory` and its strict routing refusals.
+- Produces (IF-15): strict + not approved → no client is built for either provider, `is_available=False`, `readiness() == (False, "disabled: LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED is false")`, `generate()` raises `LLMUnavailableError(reason="disabled")`; strict + approved + missing configuration → `initialize()` raises `LLMConfigurationError` whose message lists the missing variable names and no values (strict environments run only `LLM_PROVIDER=bedrock` from WS-09.18, so the name is `BEDROCK_MODEL_ID`; the Bedrock route has no API key, its credentials come from the host's instance role); outside strict the approval flag is not required and missing configuration disables the client. `readiness()` returns `(True, "ok")` when available, `(False, "disabled: missing BEDROCK_MODEL_ID")` for an unconfigured Bedrock client and `(False, "disabled: missing LLM_PORTKEY_API_KEY")` for an unconfigured Portkey client (development and test). Service lanes register it with `runtime.register_readiness_check("llm", client.readiness, critical=False)` (CR-7).
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_057_llm_settings_egress.py`
+```python
+"""MVP-057: the LLM client reads only the documented names and honours LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED (D-04: strict runs Bedrock)."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import pytest
+
+from services.shared.infrastructure.llm_client import (
+    BedrockSettings,
+    LLMClientSettings,
+    LLMConfigurationError,
+    UnifiedLLMClient,
+)
+
+_NAMES = (
+    "LLM_PORTKEY_API_KEY", "LLM_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "LLM_OPENAI_API_KEY", "LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED",
+    "LLM_ENABLE_FALLBACK", "LLM_PROVIDER", "PORTKEY_API_KEY", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "OPENAI_API_KEY",
+    "BEDROCK_MODEL_ID", "BEDROCK_ENDPOINT_URL",
+)
+MODEL_ID = "test.synthetic-model-v1"  # synthetic; never a real AWS model id
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _clean_env(monkeypatch):
+    for name in _NAMES:
+        monkeypatch.delenv(name, raising=False)
+
+
+def _factory(calls):
+    def factory(**kwargs):
+        calls.append(kwargs)
+        return object()
+
+    return factory
+
+
+def _bedrock_client(calls, **bedrock):
+    return UnifiedLLMClient(
+        LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None),
+        bedrock_settings=BedrockSettings(**bedrock),
+        bedrock_client_factory=_factory(calls),
+    )
+
+
+async def test_mvp_057_undocumented_names_are_never_read(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", "test")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("PORTKEY_API_KEY", "pk-unprefixed-value")  # the name STAGING-BRINGUP documents
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "sk-unprefixed-value")  # the name .env.example documents
+    calls = []
+    client = UnifiedLLMClient(LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None), client_factory=_factory(calls))
+    await client.initialize()
+    assert client.is_available is False
+    assert calls == []
+    assert await client.readiness() == (False, "disabled: missing LLM_PORTKEY_API_KEY")
+
+
+async def test_mvp_057_strict_approved_requires_bedrock_model_id(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", "production")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_PROVIDER", "bedrock")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED", "true")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("PORTKEY_API_KEY", "pk-unprefixed-value")
+    calls = []
+    client = _bedrock_client(calls)
+    with pytest.raises(LLMConfigurationError) as exc:
+        await client.initialize()
+    assert "BEDROCK_MODEL_ID" in str(exc.value)
+    assert "pk-unprefixed-value" not in str(exc.value)
+    assert calls == []
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("env", ["staging", "production"])
+async def test_mvp_057_strict_not_approved_is_disabled_and_reported(monkeypatch, env):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", env)
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_PROVIDER", "bedrock")
+    calls = []
+    client = _bedrock_client(calls, model_id=MODEL_ID)
+    await client.initialize()
+    assert client.is_available is False
+    assert calls == []
+    assert await client.readiness() == (False, "disabled: LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED is false")
+
+
+async def test_mvp_057_strict_approved_bedrock_is_available(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", "staging")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_PROVIDER", "bedrock")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_PHI_EGRESS_APPROVED", "true")
+    calls = []
+    client = _bedrock_client(calls, model_id=MODEL_ID)
+    await client.initialize()
+    assert client.is_available is True
+    assert len(calls) == 1
+    assert await client.readiness() == (True, "ok")
+
+
+async def test_mvp_057_bedrock_without_model_id_is_reported(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", "test")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_PROVIDER", "bedrock")
+    client = _bedrock_client([])
+    await client.initialize()
+    assert await client.readiness() == (False, "disabled: missing BEDROCK_MODEL_ID")
+
+
+async def test_mvp_057_documented_names_reach_the_gateway(monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", "test")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_PORTKEY_API_KEY", "pk-test")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("LLM_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "sk-test")
+    calls = []
+    client = UnifiedLLMClient(LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None), client_factory=_factory(calls))
+    await client.initialize()
+    assert client.is_available is True
+    assert calls[0]["api_key"] == "pk-test"
+    assert calls[0]["config"]["targets"][0]["api_key"] == "sk-test"
+    assert await client.readiness() == (True, "ok")
+
+
+def test_mvp_057_egress_flag_defaults_to_false():
+    assert LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None).phi_egress_approved is False
+```

+Run: `$env:PYTHONPATH="D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot;D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot\src"; $env:ENVIRONMENT="test"; & "D:\tmp\mvp-exec\venv\Scripts\python.exe" -m pytest tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_057_llm_settings_egress.py -o "addopts=" -p no:logging -q`

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-09-phi-codec-audit-llm.md -->
+### Task WS-09.5: Gateway caching off by default and never shared across users
+
+**Covers:** MVP-056
+**Severity:** high
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-09.4
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** security-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-04 (decided 2026-10-19; this part is unchanged from the default: `LLM_ENABLE_CACHING=false` for every request; a semantic cache is never used; strict environments refuse caching outright; the Bedrock route from WS-09.18 never caches, so `LLM_ENABLE_CACHING=true` is refused with `LLM_PROVIDER=bedrock` as well)
+**Real infra:** no
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `services/shared/infrastructure/llm_client.py` (`LLMClientSettings.enable_caching` default `False`, `cache_mode` default `"simple"`; a `model_validator(mode="after")` rejecting `cache_mode != "simple"` and rejecting `enable_caching=True` in strict environments or with `provider == "bedrock"`; `_build_portkey_config(self, cache_namespace: str | None = None)` adds `config["cache"] = {"mode": "simple"}` only when caching is enabled and a namespace is given; per-namespace clients built through `client_factory(..., cache_namespace=ns)` and kept in an LRU of at most 256 entries; requests without `metadata["user_id"]` use the uncached client)
+- Test: `tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_056_llm_caching.py` (create)
+
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: `client_factory` from WS-09.2.
+- Produces (IF-15): cache namespace format `f"solace:user:{metadata['user_id']}"`; validator errors mention "semantic" or "LLM_ENABLE_CACHING"; the default factory forwards `cache_namespace` to `AsyncPortkey` (portkey-ai 1.9.x constructor parameter, per the MVP-056 evidence `client.py:272`); per-namespace clients exist only on the Portkey route, which D-04 limits to development and test.
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_056_llm_caching.py`
+```python
+"""MVP-056: Portkey caching is off by default, never semantic and never shared across users."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+
+import pytest
+from pydantic import SecretStr
+
+from services.shared.infrastructure.llm_client import LLMClientSettings, UnifiedLLMClient
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def _env(monkeypatch):
+    for name in ("LLM_ENABLE_CACHING", "LLM_CACHE_MODE", "LLM_PROVIDER"):
+        monkeypatch.delenv(name, raising=False)
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", "test")
+
+
+def test_mvp_056_caching_off_by_default():
+    settings = LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None)
+    assert settings.enable_caching is False
+    assert settings.cache_mode == "simple"
+    assert "cache" not in UnifiedLLMClient(settings)._build_portkey_config()
+
+
+def test_mvp_056_semantic_cache_rejected():
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="semantic"):
+        LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None, enable_caching=True, cache_mode="semantic")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("env", ["staging", "production"])
+def test_mvp_056_caching_refused_in_strict(monkeypatch, env):
+    monkeypatch.setenv("ENVIRONMENT", env)
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="LLM_ENABLE_CACHING"):
+        LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None, enable_caching=True, cache_mode="simple")
+
+
+def test_mvp_056_caching_refused_with_bedrock():
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="LLM_ENABLE_CACHING"):
+        LLMClientSettings(_env_file=None, provider="bedrock", enable_caching=True, cache_mode="simple")
+
+
+async def test_mvp_056_cache_namespace_is_per_user():
+    created = []
+
+    def factory(**kwargs):
+        created.append(kwargs)
+
+        async def create(**_request):
+            content = f"r:{kwargs.get('cache_namespace')}"
+            return SimpleNamespace(choices=[SimpleNamespace(message=SimpleNamespace(content=content))])
+
+        return SimpleNamespace(chat=SimpleNamespace(completions=SimpleNamespace(create=create)))
+
+    settings = LLMClientSettings(
+        _env_file=None, portkey_api_key=SecretStr("pk-test"), enable_caching=True, cache_mode="simple"
+    )
+    client = UnifiedLLMClient(settings, client_factory=factory)
+    await client.initialize()
+    a = await client.generate(system_prompt="s", user_message="hi", service_name="t", metadata={"user_id": "user-a"})
+    b = await client.generate(system_prompt="s", user_message="hi", service_name="t", metadata={"user_id": "user-b"})
+    anon = await client.generate(system_prompt="s", user_message="hi", service_name="t")
+    assert (a, b, anon) == ("r:solace:user:user-a", "r:solace:user:user-b", "r:None")
+    namespaced = [kw for kw in created if kw.get("cache_namespace")]
+    assert {kw["cache_namespace"] for kw in namespaced} == {"solace:user:user-a", "solace:user:user-b"}
+    assert all(kw["config"]["cache"] == {"mode": "simple"} for kw in namespaced)
+    assert all("cache" not in kw["config"] for kw in created if not kw.get("cache_namespace"))
+```

+Run: `$env:PYTHONPATH="D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot;D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot\src"; $env:ENVIRONMENT="test"; & "D:\tmp\mvp-exec\venv\Scripts\python.exe" -m pytest tests/integration/phi-codec-audit-llm/test_mvp_056_llm_caching.py -o "addopts=" -p no:logging -q`
+Expected: FAIL. `test_mvp_056_caching_off_by_default` fails on `assert settings.enable_caching is False` (default True, mode "semantic", llm_client.py:37-38, and `config["cache"] == {"mode": "semantic"}` with no namespace); the validator tests fail with `DID NOT RAISE` (`test_mvp_056_caching_refused_with_bedrock` too: WS-09.18's Bedrock route ignores the caching settings but nothing refuses them); the namespace test fails because every request shares one client (`r:None` for all three).
+- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** the settings validator, `_build_portkey_config(self, cache_namespace: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]` and `_client_for(self, metadata: Mapping[str, Any] | None)` used by `_execute_completion`. The uncached base client is the one built in `initialize()`; namespaced clients are created lazily with the same `api_key`/`base_url` and `config=self._build_portkey_config(cache_namespace=ns)`.

Expected: all pass except the WS-09.3 node ids plus, in `services/therapy_service/tests/test_llm_client.py`, `TestLLMClientSettings::test_default_settings`, `TestUnifiedLLMClient::test_build_portkey_config_with_caching` and `TestUnifiedLLMClient::test_get_statistics` (they assert the semantic default; CR-5). +<!-- brief extracted from WS-12-safety-detection-lexicon.md -->
+### Task WS-12.2: solace_clinical core: normalization, max-only fusion and versioned crisis resources
+**Covers:** MVP-030
+**Severity:** blocker
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** none
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** safety-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-05 (default assumed: max-only fusion; LLM, fallback and stub signals may raise but never lower a level; owner acceptance recorded as a ledger Ruling until clinician sign-off) and D-29 (decided 2026-10-19: several countries, US first. Resources are a per-locale, versioned registry; only en-US is populated, with exactly today's set (988 Suicide and Crisis Lifeline, call or text 988; Crisis Text Line, text HOME to 741741; 911); every other or unknown locale gets a generic fallback (the local emergency number plus an international helpline directory), never an empty list and never an error, logged without PHI and counted by a metric. Each further country is added only with clinician-verified resources before its users join; the fallback copy is provisional until the D-29 clinician sign-off)
+**Real infra:** no
+**Files:**
+- Create: `src/solace_clinical/__init__.py`, `src/solace_clinical/normalize.py`, `src/solace_clinical/fusion.py`, `src/solace_clinical/resources.py`, `src/solace_clinical/escalation_reason.py`
+- Test: `tests/clinical/test_solace_clinical_core.py`
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: `solace_common.enums.CrisisLevel` (frozen values, WS-07).
+- Produces (IF-07): `normalize_text(text: str) -> str`; `CATEGORIES: frozenset[str]` = {suicidal_ideation, suicide_plan, means_access, self_harm, overdose, homicidal_ideation, abuse_disclosure, imminent_danger, hopelessness}; `@dataclass(frozen=True) RiskSignal(source: str, level: CrisisLevel, confidence: float, reliable: bool)`; `fuse_risk(signals: Sequence[RiskSignal]) -> CrisisLevel`; `crisis_resources(locale: str | None = "en-US") -> list[dict]` with keys `name, contact, kind` (fresh copies; `crisis_resources("en-US")` and `crisis_resources()` return exactly today's three en-US entries); `RESOURCES_VERSION: str = "1.0.0"`, kept as the en-US version (`RESOURCE_VERSIONS[DEFAULT_LOCALE]`) so every existing en-US assertion, WS-05.6's `resources_version == RESOURCES_VERSION` included, stays true. New: `level_rank(level: CrisisLevel) -> int` (NONE 0 ... CRITICAL 4).
+- Produces (D-29 locale registry, all in `solace_clinical.resources` and re-exported; the one locale contract WS-08, WS-10, WS-11, WS-14 and WS-15 use): `DEFAULT_LOCALE: str = "en-US"`; `UNDETERMINED_LOCALE: str = "und"`; `supported_locales() -> frozenset[str]` (today `{"en-US"}`); `RESOURCE_VERSIONS: Mapping[str, str]` (read-only, today `{"en-US": "1.0.0"}`; one version per locale); `FALLBACK_RESOURCES_VERSION: str = "fallback-1.0.0"`; `canonical_locale(tag: object) -> str | None` (exactly WS-08.7's `normalize_locale` grammar `^[a-z]{2,3}([-_][A-Za-z]{2})?$`, case-insensitive, returning `ll` or `ll-CC`; `None` for anything else, non-`str` included; never raises); `resolve_locale(claim: object, requested: object = None) -> str` (a `None` or blank claim is `DEFAULT_LOCALE`, so a missing token claim means en-US; a malformed claim is `UNDETERMINED_LOCALE`; `requested` is honoured only when it is in `supported_locales()` and keeps the claim's region, so a request-supplied locale can refine the language but never leave the supported set or change the country; never raises); `@dataclass(frozen=True) CrisisResourceSet(locale: str, resources: tuple[Mapping[str, str], ...], version: str, fallback: bool)` (`locale` is the registry key served, or `"fallback"`); `crisis_resource_set(locale: object = DEFAULT_LOCALE) -> CrisisResourceSet` (the call that serves, logs and counts; callers that need both the list and the version call it once); `resources_version(locale: object = DEFAULT_LOCALE) -> str` (a pure lookup: the locale's version or `FALLBACK_RESOURCES_VERSION`; no log, no count). Fallback (provisional copy, D-29 sign-off): `[{"name": "Local emergency services", "contact": "Call your local emergency number if you are in immediate danger", "kind": "phone"}, {"name": "Find A Helpline (international directory)", "contact": "https://findahelpline.com", "kind": "url"}]`. Every fallback served logs WARNING `crisis_resources_locale_fallback` with `reason` in {`unsupported_locale` (well-formed, not in the registry), `unknown_locale` (malformed or `und`)} and, only for a well-formed tag other than `und`, `locale_tag` (a language or country code, never user data), and increments the counter `CRISIS_RESOURCES_FALLBACK` (`solace_crisis_resources_fallback_total{reason}`, prometheus_client, registered in this module; the alert rule is R-12-11). No setting is introduced: `DEFAULT_LOCALE` is a clinical constant like the D-06 watch values, not an environment variable.
+- Produces (IF-06 `escalation_reason` format, crisis-review F10; the one definition WS-12.8, WS-12.9, WS-05.7, WS-06.12 and WS-13.6 use): `format_escalation_reason(source: Literal["auto", "manual"], level: str, categories: Iterable[str]) -> str` (`"<source>:<LEVEL>"`, plus `":" + ",".join(sorted codes in CATEGORIES)` when any remain; other values are dropped); `parse_escalation_reason(reason: str) -> tuple[str, str, tuple[str, ...]] | None` (`(source, level, sorted codes)` when the whole string matches `^(auto|manual):(NONE|LOW|ELEVATED|MEDIUM|HIGH|CRITICAL)(:[a-z_]+(,[a-z_]+)*)?$` and every code is in `CATEGORIES`, else `None`; total over every `str`, never raises). `"unspecified"` is the only other value an `escalation_reason` may carry.
+

`RESOURCE_VERSIONS`, `RESOURCES_VERSION = RESOURCE_VERSIONS[DEFAULT_LOCALE]` and `supported_locales()` derive from `_REGISTRY`, so adding a country is one clinician-verified entry with its own version (the add-a-locale rule WS-12.3 records in CLINICAL-VALIDATION §11). `canonical_locale` uses the compiled pattern with `re.IGNORECASE` and returns `lang.lower()` plus `"-" + region.upper()`; `crisis_resource_set` resolves `None` or blank to `DEFAULT_LOCALE`, serves the registry entry of a canonical supported tag, and otherwise serves the fallback, logging the WARNING through `structlog.get_logger("solace_clinical")` and incrementing `CRISIS_RESOURCES_FALLBACK.labels(reason=...)` inside `try/except Exception: pass`, so serving resources never raises and never depends on the LLM, a downstream service, Kafka or the database. +### Task WS-12.3: Crisis lexicon v1, screen_text, the 200+ phrase set and the clinical record
+**Covers:** MVP-084, MVP-182, MVP-086, MVP-178
+**Severity:** high
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-12.2
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** safety-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-05 (default assumed: provisional lexicon v1 built from safety's keyword lists, patterns and `clinical_lexicon.json` plus every register gap; owner acceptance recorded; named clinician signs before W5), D-06 (episode semantics recorded for review), D-29
+**Real infra:** no

New: `solace_clinical.phrases.load_phrase_set(path: str | os.PathLike | None = None) -> PhraseSet` (default `<repo>/tests/clinical/crisis_phrases_v1.yaml`, resolved from the module file), `PhraseSet(version: str, positive: tuple[PositivePhrase, ...], negative: tuple[NegativePhrase, ...])`, `PositivePhrase(text, min_level: CrisisLevel, categories: frozenset[str])`, `NegativePhrase(text, max_level: CrisisLevel)`, `PhraseFailure(text, kind, expected, actual)`, `check_detector(detect: Callable[[str], CrisisLevel | tuple[CrisisLevel, Iterable[str]]], phrase_set: PhraseSet | None = None) -> list[PhraseFailure]` and `async check_detector_async(detect, phrase_set=None) -> list[PhraseFailure]` (categories are checked only when `detect` returns a tuple). WS-10, WS-15, WS-16 and WS-17 call `check_detector[_async]` from their own suites.
