+```python
+# tests/clinical/test_crisis_phrases_v1.py
+from collections import Counter
+import pytest
+from solace_clinical import CATEGORIES, LEXICON_VERSION, level_rank, screen_text
+from solace_clinical.lexicon import load_lexicon
+from solace_clinical.phrases import load_phrase_set
+from solace_common.enums import CrisisLevel as L
+
+pytestmark = pytest.mark.clinical
+PHRASES = load_phrase_set()
+REQUIRED_POSITIVE = {  # register regressions: text -> minimum level
+    "I want to kill myself": L.CRITICAL, "I'm suicidal": L.HIGH,
+    "I've been thinking about ending my life": L.HIGH, "I'm going to jump off the bridge tonight": L.CRITICAL,
+    "I took all my pills": L.CRITICAL, "I have been saving up pills and I know how": L.HIGH,
+    "I feel like giving up, I am a burden": L.HIGH, "I wrote a goodbye letter and gave away my things": L.HIGH,
+    "It isn't fair. I'd be better off dead": L.CRITICAL, "I don't know. Suicide maybe": L.HIGH,
+    "Things aren't getting better. Overdose seems easiest": L.HIGH,
+    "everyone would be better off without me": L.HIGH, "I can't take this anymore, nobody would miss me": L.HIGH,
+    "I can\u2019t go on": L.HIGH, "I don\u2019t want to be alive anymore": L.HIGH,
+    "I\u2019m going to end it tonight": L.CRITICAL, "I want to kill my stepfather": L.HIGH,
+    "my partner hits me and I'm scared to go home": L.HIGH, "he has a knife and he is outside my door": L.CRITICAL,
+    "I want to die :-} bye": L.CRITICAL, "nobody would miss me }": L.HIGH,
+    "I cut myself again last night": L.HIGH, "I feel hopeless": L.ELEVATED,
+    "I'm so overwhelmed": L.LOW,  # general distress is hopelessness LOW; the WS-12.6 crisis-watch test relies on it
+}
+REQUIRED_NEGATIVE = {  # text -> maximum level
+    "Hi, how are you?": L.NONE, "Thanks, that helps": L.NONE, "I am cutting vegetables for dinner": L.LOW,
+    "I'm cutting back on coffee": L.LOW, "I could kill for a coffee": L.LOW,
+    "I need to pick up my pills from the pharmacy": L.LOW, "We tied the boat with a rope": L.LOW,
+    "My dad has a gun collection": L.LOW, "I bought a knife and some rope for camping by the bridge": L.LOW,
+    "Long week: tired, stressed, worried, upset, frustrated and exhausted": L.ELEVATED,
+    "We discussed harm reduction strategies": L.NONE, "I'm not suicidal, I just had a rough day": L.LOW,
+    "This traffic is killing me": L.LOW,
+}
+ADVERSARIAL_POSITIVE = {  # review focus 1: disclosures outside plain wording -> minimum level (provisional, D-05)
+    "i want to k1ll mys3lf": L.CRITICAL, "I want to kil myself": L.CRITICAL,          # leetspeak, misspelling
+    "im sucidal": L.HIGH, "i feel suicidle lately": L.HIGH,                           # misspellings
+    "honestly i just wanna unalive myself": L.CRITICAL, "i'm gonna kms tonight": L.HIGH,  # slang
+    "I want to die \U0001F62D\U0001F62D": L.CRITICAL, "\U0001F494 i can't go on anymore \U0001F494": L.HIGH,  # emoji
+    "me quiero matar": L.CRITICAL, "ya no quiero vivir": L.HIGH,                      # Spanish (US pilot)
+    "honestly I just want to morir": L.HIGH,                                          # code-switched
+    "what if I just ended it all": L.HIGH,                                            # hypothetical
+    "she asked if I ever think 'I want to kill myself' and I said yes": L.CRITICAL,   # quoted speech
+    "I don't know what to do and I've been thinking about ending my life": L.HIGH,     # earlier negation, long clause
+    ("I'm not going to pretend I'm fine, work is awful and my family isn't talking to me and "
+     "honestly I keep thinking I'd be better off dead"): L.CRITICAL,
+}
+ADVERSARIAL_NEGATIVE = {"I drove 20 kms to work today": L.LOW, "that joke killed me \U0001F602": L.LOW,
+                        "no quiero ir a trabajar hoy": L.LOW}
+
+def test_phrase_set_is_large_and_balanced():
+    assert len(PHRASES.positive) + len(PHRASES.negative) >= 200 and len(PHRASES.negative) >= 60
+    per_category = Counter(c for p in PHRASES.positive for c in p.categories)
+    assert all(per_category[c] >= 8 for c in CATEGORIES)
+    assert sum("\u2019" in p.text for p in PHRASES.positive) >= 20
+
+def test_register_regressions_are_pinned_in_the_set():
+    pos = {p.text: p.min_level for p in PHRASES.positive}
+    neg = {n.text: n.max_level for n in PHRASES.negative}
+    assert all(level_rank(pos[t]) >= level_rank(lvl) for t, lvl in REQUIRED_POSITIVE.items())
+    assert all(level_rank(neg[t]) <= level_rank(lvl) for t, lvl in REQUIRED_NEGATIVE.items())
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("phrase", PHRASES.positive, ids=lambda p: p.text[:40])
+def test_positive_phrase_reaches_min_level_and_categories(phrase):
+    result = screen_text(phrase.text)
+    assert level_rank(result.level) >= level_rank(phrase.min_level)
+    assert phrase.categories <= result.categories
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("phrase", PHRASES.negative, ids=lambda p: p.text[:40])
+def test_negative_phrase_stays_at_or_below_max_level(phrase):
+    assert level_rank(screen_text(phrase.text).level) <= level_rank(phrase.max_level)
+
+def test_negation_is_clause_bounded_and_never_hits_intent():
+    assert screen_text("It isn't fair. I'd be better off dead").level == L.CRITICAL
+    assert screen_text("I'm not going to lie, I want to kill myself").level == L.CRITICAL
+    negated = screen_text("I'm not suicidal")
+    assert level_rank(negated.level) <= level_rank(L.LOW) and negated.negated_rule_ids
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("text,floor", ADVERSARIAL_POSITIVE.items(), ids=lambda v: str(v)[:40])
+def test_review_focus_1_adversarial_disclosures_reach_their_floor(text, floor):
+    assert level_rank(screen_text(text).level) >= level_rank(floor)
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("text,ceiling", ADVERSARIAL_NEGATIVE.items(), ids=lambda v: str(v)[:40])
+def test_review_focus_1_adversarial_benign_text_stays_low(text, ceiling):
+    assert level_rank(screen_text(text).level) <= level_rank(ceiling)
+
+def test_review_focus_1_adversarial_cases_are_in_the_shared_phrase_set():
+    pos = {p.text: p.min_level for p in PHRASES.positive}
+    neg = {n.text: n.max_level for n in PHRASES.negative}
+    assert all(t in pos and level_rank(pos[t]) >= level_rank(lvl) for t, lvl in ADVERSARIAL_POSITIVE.items())
+    assert all(t in neg and level_rank(neg[t]) <= level_rank(lvl) for t, lvl in ADVERSARIAL_NEGATIVE.items())
+
+def test_rule_ids_are_codes_and_metadata_is_present():
+    result = screen_text("I want to kill myself tonight")
+    assert result.rule_ids and all(" " not in rid for rid in result.rule_ids)
+    meta = load_lexicon().metadata
+    assert meta["version"] == LEXICON_VERSION == result.lexicon_version and meta["jurisdiction"] == "US"
+    assert {"approved_by", "approved_at"} <= meta.keys()
+```

Algorithm: normalize; replace every symbol character (Unicode categories So and Sk, which covers emoji) with a space; screen two variants and keep the maximum: the text itself and a de-leeted copy in which `0 1 3 4 5 7 @ $` become `o i e a s t a s` inside tokens that also contain a letter (so "20 kms" is unchanged); split each variant into clauses on `[.!?;:\n]`, commas, ` - ` and the words `but`, `however`, `though`, `although`, `yet`; for each rule whose literal anchor (the longest literal fragment of a pattern, precomputed at load) occurs in the variant, run its compiled patterns; a match counts unless `negation_policy == "clause"` and a negation cue (`not`, `no`, `never`, `don't`, `doesn't`, `didn't`, `isn't`, `aren't`, `wasn't`, `won't`, `wouldn't`, `no longer`, `denies`, `without`) sits within the three tokens immediately before the match in the same clause (an earlier, unrelated negation in a long clause never suppresses a later disclosure; hypothetical frames such as `what if` and `if i` are not cues); a rule with `requires_first_person: true` counts only when its clause contains `i`, `i'm`, `i've`, `i'd`, `i'll`, `me`, `my`, `myself` or `mine`. Level is the maximum matched rule level; categories, rule ids and negated rule ids are sorted and deduplicated. +### Task WS-12.4: Slot 014 escalation decision columns (one auto decision per turn)
+**Covers:** MVP-081, MVP-088, MVP-177, MVP-181
+**Severity:** high
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** none
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** safety-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-01 (default assumed: Alembic is the schema of record; owners edit only `upgrade()`/`downgrade()` of their slot)
+**Real infra:** yes
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `migrations/versions/014_safety.py` (`upgrade()`/`downgrade()` only; revision ids untouched)
+- Test: `tests/realinfra/safety-detection-lexicon/test_mvp_088_slot014_escalations.py`
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: IF-03 slot `014_safety` on the linear chain; IF-19 `pg_migrated -> PgHandle(dsn_app, dsn_migrator, dsn_superuser, app_pool)`.
+- Produces: `escalations` gains `correlation_id UUID NULL`, `source VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto'` with `CHECK (source IN ('auto','manual'))`, `categories TEXT[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'`, `detection_layer SMALLINT NULL`, `encryption_key_id VARCHAR(64) NULL`, `pseudonymized_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`; index `ix_escalations_correlation_id`; partial unique index `ux_escalations_auto_correlation ON escalations (correlation_id) WHERE source = 'auto' AND correlation_id IS NOT NULL`. `escalations` stays non-RLS (IF-03).
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `tests/realinfra/safety-detection-lexicon/test_mvp_088_slot014_escalations.py`
+```python
+import uuid
+from datetime import UTC, datetime
+import asyncpg
+import pytest
+
+pytestmark = [pytest.mark.realinfra, pytest.mark.integration]
+INSERT = ("INSERT INTO escalations (escalation_id, user_id, crisis_level, priority, status, reason, created_at, "
+          "correlation_id, source, categories) VALUES ($1,$2,'CRITICAL','CRITICAL','PENDING','auto:CRITICAL',$3,$4,$5,$6)")
+
+async def test_mvp_088_slot014_adds_decision_columns(pg_migrated):
+    async with pg_migrated.app_pool.acquire() as conn:
+        rows = await conn.fetch("SELECT column_name, data_type FROM information_schema.columns "
+                                "WHERE table_name = 'escalations'")
+    cols = {r["column_name"]: r["data_type"] for r in rows}
+    assert {"correlation_id", "source", "categories", "detection_layer", "encryption_key_id",
+            "pseudonymized_at"} <= cols.keys()
+    assert cols["categories"] == "ARRAY" and cols["correlation_id"] == "uuid"
+
+async def test_mvp_177_one_auto_decision_per_turn(pg_migrated):
+    turn, user = uuid.uuid4(), uuid.uuid4()
+    async with pg_migrated.app_pool.acquire() as conn:
+        await conn.execute(INSERT, uuid.uuid4(), user, datetime.now(UTC), turn, "auto", ["suicidal_ideation"])
+        with pytest.raises(asyncpg.UniqueViolationError):
+            await conn.execute(INSERT, uuid.uuid4(), user, datetime.now(UTC), turn, "auto", [])
+        await conn.execute(INSERT, uuid.uuid4(), user, datetime.now(UTC), turn, "manual", [])  # allowed
+        with pytest.raises(asyncpg.CheckViolationError):
+            await conn.execute(INSERT, uuid.uuid4(), user, datetime.now(UTC), uuid.uuid4(), "robot", [])
+```

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-13-notification-alert-delivery.md -->
+### Task WS-13.3: Slot 015: crisis episodes, one-time acknowledgement tokens, risk windows and body-less delivery records
+**Covers:** MVP-082, MVP-150
+**Severity:** high
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-02.3, WS-12.4 (phase A schema lane: slot 015 runs after slot 014), WS-05.2
+**Implementer model:** opus
+**Review:** safety-critical
+**Decision gate:** D-01 (default assumed: Alembic is the only schema of record; the owner edits only `upgrade()`/`downgrade()`), D-24 (default assumed: crisis episodes are kept and pseudonymized, so `pseudonymized_at` exists from W1)
+**Real infra:** yes
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `migrations/versions/015_notification.py` (`upgrade()` and `downgrade()` only, behind the `_is_postgres()` guard used by slots 002-009)
+- Test: `tests/realinfra/notification-alert-delivery/test_mvp_150_slot_015_schema.py`; regression `tests/migrations/test_chain_real_pg.py` (WS-02)
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: IF-03 roles and default privileges from slot 009; tables `notifications` and `delivery_attempts` from 001.
+- Produces (IF-09 columns plus additions under New interfaces):
+  - `crisis_alerts(alert_id uuid PK, user_id uuid NOT NULL (no FK, so pseudonyms fit), opened_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), last_event_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), max_level varchar(16) NOT NULL CHECK IN ('HIGH','CRITICAL'), status varchar(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK IN ('open','acknowledged','resolved'), correlation_ids uuid[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', sources text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', categories text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', pages_requested int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, page_count int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, repage_count int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, last_paged_at timestamptz, ack_token_hash char(64), acknowledged_by uuid, acknowledged_at timestamptz, resolved_by uuid, resolved_at timestamptz, last_update_sent_at timestamptz, pseudonymized_at timestamptz)`, indexes `ix_crisis_alerts_user_last_event (user_id, last_event_at DESC)`, `ix_crisis_alerts_open (status) WHERE status = 'open'`, GIN `ix_crisis_alerts_correlation_ids (correlation_ids)`. Columns `user_id`, `acknowledged_by` (NULL for an anonymous link acknowledgement) and `acknowledged_at` are readable by solace_app, as WS-07.8 requires (request from WS-07).
+  - `crisis_alert_ack_tokens(token_hash char(64) PK, alert_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES crisis_alerts ON DELETE CASCADE, recipient_hash char(64) NOT NULL, issued_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL, used_at timestamptz)`, index on `alert_id`.
+  - `risk_alert_windows(user_id uuid PK, window_started_at timestamptz NOT NULL, last_event_at timestamptz NOT NULL, max_level varchar(16) NOT NULL, assessment_count int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, categories text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', status varchar(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK IN ('pending','sent','failed'), attempts int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, sent_at timestamptz)`.
+  - `notifications`: `user_id`, `title` and `body` DROP NOT NULL; ADD `alert_id uuid REFERENCES crisis_alerts ON DELETE SET NULL`, `page_seq int`, `recipient_hash char(64)`, `recipient_role varchar(16)`, `subject_user_id uuid`, `kind varchar(32)`; `UNIQUE INDEX ux_notifications_alert_page_recipient (alert_id, page_seq, recipient_hash, channel) WHERE alert_id IS NOT NULL`; index `ix_notifications_subject_user_id`.
+  - All four tables are non-RLS (IF-03 list extended under New interfaces). `downgrade()` drops the new indexes, columns and tables in reverse order and runs `DELETE FROM notifications WHERE user_id IS NULL OR title IS NULL OR body IS NULL` before restoring NOT NULL.
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `tests/realinfra/notification-alert-delivery/test_mvp_150_slot_015_schema.py`
+```python
+"""MVP-082 / MVP-150 (IF-09, IF-03): slot 015 stores episodes, one-time tokens, risk windows and body-less records for solace_app."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from uuid import uuid4
+
+import asyncpg
+import pytest
+
+pytestmark = [pytest.mark.realinfra, pytest.mark.integration]
+
+EPISODE_COLUMNS = {"alert_id", "user_id", "opened_at", "last_event_at", "max_level", "status", "correlation_ids",
+                   "sources", "categories", "pages_requested", "page_count", "repage_count", "last_paged_at",
+                   "ack_token_hash", "acknowledged_by", "acknowledged_at", "resolved_by", "resolved_at",
+                   "last_update_sent_at", "pseudonymized_at"}
+INSERT_EPISODE = ("INSERT INTO crisis_alerts (alert_id, user_id, max_level, correlation_ids, sources, categories) "
+                  "VALUES ($1, $2, 'CRITICAL', $3, $4, $5)")
+
+
+async def _columns(pg, table: str) -> dict[str, tuple[str, str]]:
+    rows = await pg.superuser_fetch(
+        "SELECT column_name, data_type, is_nullable FROM information_schema.columns "
+        "WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = $1", table)
+    return {r["column_name"]: (r["data_type"], r["is_nullable"]) for r in rows}
+
+
+async def test_mvp_082_crisis_alerts_has_the_if09_columns(pg_migrated) -> None:
+    cols = await _columns(pg_migrated, "crisis_alerts")
+    assert EPISODE_COLUMNS <= cols.keys()
+    assert cols["correlation_ids"][0] == "ARRAY" and cols["sources"][0] == "ARRAY"
+    assert cols["acknowledged_by"][1] == "YES" and cols["user_id"][1] == "NO"
+
+
+async def test_mvp_082_app_role_writes_episodes_without_rls_context(pg_migrated) -> None:
+    alert, user, turn = uuid4(), uuid4(), uuid4()
+    async with pg_migrated.app_pool.acquire() as conn:
+        await conn.execute(INSERT_EPISODE, alert, user, [turn], ["safety-service"], ["suicidal_ideation"])
+        row = await conn.fetchrow("SELECT status, page_count, pages_requested, repage_count, correlation_ids "
+                                  "FROM crisis_alerts WHERE alert_id = $1", alert)
+    assert (row["status"], row["page_count"], row["pages_requested"], row["repage_count"]) == ("open", 0, 0, 0)
+    assert row["correlation_ids"] == [turn]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("column,value", [("status", "closed"), ("max_level", "ELEVATED")])
+async def test_mvp_082_status_and_level_are_constrained(pg_migrated, column: str, value: str) -> None:
+    async with pg_migrated.app_pool.acquire() as conn:
+        await conn.execute(INSERT_EPISODE, alert := uuid4(), uuid4(), [], [], [])
+        with pytest.raises(asyncpg.exceptions.CheckViolationError):
+            await conn.execute(f"UPDATE crisis_alerts SET {column} = $1 WHERE alert_id = $2", value, alert)
+
+
+async def test_mvp_082_ack_tokens_are_unique_and_cascade_with_the_episode(pg_migrated) -> None:
+    alert, token = uuid4(), "a" * 64
+    async with pg_migrated.app_pool.acquire() as conn:
+        await conn.execute(INSERT_EPISODE, alert, uuid4(), [], [], [])
+        insert = ("INSERT INTO crisis_alert_ack_tokens (token_hash, alert_id, recipient_hash, expires_at) "
+                  "VALUES ($1, $2, $3, now() + interval '24 hours')")
+        await conn.execute(insert, token, alert, "b" * 64)
+        with pytest.raises(asyncpg.exceptions.UniqueViolationError):
+            await conn.execute(insert, token, alert, "c" * 64)
+        await conn.execute("DELETE FROM crisis_alerts WHERE alert_id = $1", alert)
+        assert await conn.fetchval("SELECT count(*) FROM crisis_alert_ack_tokens WHERE alert_id = $1", alert) == 0
+
+
+async def test_mvp_150_bodyless_alert_records_are_unique_per_recipient_and_page(pg_migrated) -> None:
+    alert, user = uuid4(), uuid4()
+    insert = ("INSERT INTO notifications (id, created_at, updated_at, category, channel, status, template_id, alert_id, "
+              "page_seq, recipient_hash, recipient_role, subject_user_id, kind) VALUES "
+              "($1, now(), now(), 'safety', 'email', 'PENDING', 'crisis_alert', $2, 1, $3, 'on_call', $4, 'crisis_page')")
+    async with pg_migrated.app_pool.acquire() as conn:
+        await conn.execute(INSERT_EPISODE, alert, user, [], [], [])
+        first = uuid4()
+        await conn.execute(insert, first, alert, "d" * 64, user)
+        with pytest.raises(asyncpg.exceptions.UniqueViolationError):
+            await conn.execute(insert, uuid4(), alert, "d" * 64, user)
+        await conn.execute("INSERT INTO delivery_attempts (id, created_at, updated_at, notification_id, channel, "
+                           "attempt_number, status, attempted_at) VALUES ($1, now(), now(), $2, 'email', 1, 'DELIVERED', now())",
+                           uuid4(), first)
+        row = await conn.fetchrow("SELECT title, body, user_id FROM notifications WHERE id = $1", first)
+    assert (row["title"], row["body"], row["user_id"]) == (None, None, None)
+
+
+async def test_mvp_156_risk_alert_windows_hold_one_row_per_user(pg_migrated) -> None:
+    user = uuid4()
+    insert = ("INSERT INTO risk_alert_windows (user_id, window_started_at, last_event_at, max_level) "
+              "VALUES ($1, now(), now(), 'ELEVATED')")
+    async with pg_migrated.app_pool.acquire() as conn:
+        await conn.execute(insert, user)
+        with pytest.raises(asyncpg.exceptions.UniqueViolationError):
+            await conn.execute(insert, user)
+        with pytest.raises(asyncpg.exceptions.CheckViolationError):
+            await conn.execute("UPDATE risk_alert_windows SET status = 'queued' WHERE user_id = $1", user)
+```
+- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and confirm it fails**

+Expected: FAIL. `asyncpg.exceptions.UndefinedTableError: relation "crisis_alerts" does not exist` (no episode store exists, MVP-082) and the body-less insert fails with `NotNullViolationError` on `user_id`/`title`/`body` because 001 made them NOT NULL and nothing ever wrote these tables (MVP-150, migrations/versions/001_initial_schema.py:884-939).
+- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** the slot body per Produces with `op.execute` DDL (arrays and partial or GIN indexes are PostgreSQL-only, hence the guard). `revision` and `down_revision` stay as WS-02.3 wrote them.

Expected: all pass (upgrade head, downgrade base, upgrade head; solace_app has no DDL; every policy uses NULLIF).

+<!-- brief extracted from WS-14-accounts-roster-consent-erasure.md -->
+### Task WS-14.4: Slot 012 (W1 body): backup on-call flag and the account_tokens table
+
+**Covers:** MVP-105, MVP-230
+**Severity:** high
+**Wave:** 1
+**Depends on:** WS-02.3, WS-05.2
+**Implementer model:** sonnet
+**Review:** standard
+**Decision gate:** D-01 (default assumed: owners edit only `upgrade()`/`downgrade()`; slot bodies may be amended in place until the first real deployment). D-13 (default assumed: backup on-call clinicians exist).
+**Real infra:** yes
+
+**Files:**
+- Modify: `migrations/versions/012_user_accounts.py`. Only `upgrade()` and `downgrade()` change.
+  - `upgrade()`:
+    - `op.add_column("users", sa.Column("is_backup", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("false")))`;
+    - `op.create_table("account_tokens", token_hash String(64) PK, user_id UUID NOT NULL FK users.id ON DELETE CASCADE, purpose String(32) NOT NULL, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL server_default now(), expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL, used_at timestamptz NULL, CheckConstraint("purpose IN ('email_verification','account_setup')", name="ck_account_tokens_purpose"))`;
+    - `op.create_index("ix_account_tokens_user_purpose", "account_tokens", ["user_id", "purpose"])`.
+  - `downgrade()` reverses the three in reverse order.
+  - No RLS: the table is read before authentication. Grants come from 009's default privileges.
+- Test: `tests/realinfra/accounts-roster-consent-erasure/test_mvp_105_slot012_accounts.py` (create)
+
+**Interfaces:**
+- Consumes: the slot-012 scaffold (revision `012_user_accounts`, down revision `011_audit_trail`) and 009 default privileges (WS-02.3); IF-19 `pg_migrated`; this directory's `superuser_conn` and `seed_user` (WS-14.1).
+- Produces: `users.is_backup`, and table `account_tokens(token_hash, user_id, purpose, created_at, expires_at, used_at)` with purposes `email_verification` and `account_setup`. Consumed by WS-14.6, WS-14.8 and WS-14.9. The task runs in the W1 schema lane after slot 010 and before slots 014 and 015.
+
+- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** in `tests/realinfra/accounts-roster-consent-erasure/test_mvp_105_slot012_accounts.py`
+```python
+"""Slot 012 (W1): users.is_backup and account_tokens exist, are usable by solace_app and check their purpose."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import asyncpg
+import pytest
+
+pytestmark = [pytest.mark.realinfra, pytest.mark.integration]
+
+
+async def test_slot012_adds_backup_flag_and_account_tokens(superuser_conn) -> None:
+    users = {r["column_name"]: r for r in await superuser_conn.fetch(
+        "SELECT column_name, is_nullable, column_default FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name = 'users'")}
+    assert users["is_backup"]["is_nullable"] == "NO" and "false" in users["is_backup"]["column_default"]
+    cols = {r["column_name"] for r in await superuser_conn.fetch(
+        "SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns WHERE table_name = 'account_tokens'")}
+    assert cols == {"token_hash", "user_id", "purpose", "created_at", "expires_at", "used_at"}
+    assert await superuser_conn.fetchval("SELECT relrowsecurity FROM pg_class WHERE relname = 'account_tokens'") is False
+
+
+async def test_slot012_solace_app_uses_account_tokens_and_purpose_is_checked(pg_migrated, seed_user) -> None:
+    uid = await seed_user()
+    conn = await asyncpg.connect(pg_migrated.dsn_app)
+    try:
+        await conn.execute("INSERT INTO account_tokens (token_hash, user_id, purpose, expires_at) "
+                           "VALUES ($1, $2, 'account_setup', now() + interval '1 hour')", "a" * 64, uid)
+        assert await conn.fetchval("UPDATE account_tokens SET used_at = now() WHERE token_hash = $1 "
+                                   "AND used_at IS NULL RETURNING user_id", "a" * 64) == uid
+        with pytest.raises(asyncpg.CheckViolationError):
+            await conn.execute("INSERT INTO account_tokens (token_hash, user_id, purpose, expires_at) "
+                               "VALUES ($1, $2, 'password_reset', now())", "b" * 64, uid)
+    finally:
+        await conn.close()
+```
+- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and confirm it fails**
+Run: `$env:PYTHONPATH="D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot;D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot\src"; $env:ENVIRONMENT="test"; $env:SOLACE_REQUIRE_REALINFRA="1"; & "D:\tmp\mvp-exec\venv\Scripts\python.exe" -m pytest tests/realinfra/accounts-roster-consent-erasure/test_mvp_105_slot012_accounts.py -o "addopts=" -p no:logging -q`
+Expected: FAIL with `KeyError: 'is_backup'` and `UndefinedTableError: relation "account_tokens" does not exist`. There is no place to store a backup on-call flag (MVP-105) or an expiring, hashed one-time token (MVP-230).
+- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `upgrade()` and `downgrade()` as listed. Use `postgresql.UUID(as_uuid=True)`, as 001 does.
+- [ ] **Step 4: Run it and confirm it passes**
+Run: same command. Expected: PASS
+- [ ] **Step 5: Regression:** run the chain test (upgrade head, downgrade base, upgrade head) and this directory.
+Run:
+```
+$env:PYTHONPATH="D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot;D:\Repo\Contextual-Chatbot\src"; $env:ENVIRONMENT="test"; $env:SOLACE_REQUIRE_REALINFRA="1"
+& "D:\tmp\mvp-exec\venv\Scripts\python.exe" -m pytest tests/migrations -o "addopts=" -p no:logging -q
+& "D:\tmp\mvp-exec\venv\Scripts\python.exe" -m pytest tests/realinfra/accounts-roster-consent-erasure -o "addopts=" -p no:logging -q
+```
+Expected: all pass.
+- [ ] **Step 6: Checkpoint:** `& D:\tmp\mvp-exec\tools\checkpoint.ps1 -Label "WS-14.4:done"` and record the printed tree id in the ledger. No git commit.
+

+﻿<!-- W1-prep pre-flight scan, 2026-11-24 -->
+# W1 pre-flight conflict scan
+
+Written by the W1-prep stage (docs only). Method: a script parsed the Files, Interfaces, Depends-on and Cross-workstream lines of the 69 W1 tasks in the 18 plan files, plus the DAG mutex keys and the tracker deps, then I judged each pair. Scripts live in D:/tmp/mvp-exec/w1prep/. Tracker deps list only same-wave same-phase dependencies by design, so the task headers were also compared with the tracker.
+
+## Part 1. Pairs of W1 tasks that share a file or an interface
+
+Ordering column: how the controller keeps the pair apart. "UNORDERED" means no dependency and no phase gate, so only the mutex key stops them running together.

Same registry, compose files and tests/deploy/test_env_contract.py. |

+## Part 2. Does each W1 task agree with itself?
+
+Automated checks per task: (a) every Modify target exists on disk or is created by an earlier dependency; (b) every Create target is absent today; (c) every Test file is referenced by a Run step; (d) a RED expectation (Expected: FAIL) exists; (e) solace_* imports in the test code resolve to a module on disk or one the task creates; (f) the Depends-on header against the tracker deps; (g) cross-workstream prerequisites against the Depends-on header. "Stale tool" means the plan text names checkpoint.ps1 or D:\tmp\mvp-exec\tools, which exist only in the 2026-10 Windows workspace; the controller overrides with `mvp_exec.py checkpoint` (ledger R-12), the same in every task and not repeated.

+## Part 3. Rulings from this scan
+
+Each is also ledgered as a "Ruling:" line.
+
+| Id | Ruling | Why | Cost if wrong |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| R-1 | A1 and A2 are advisory. Dispatch by readiness, mutex keys, at most 2 real-infra lanes and chain height. The schema slot 014 task (WS-12.4) starts in round 1. | pick_ready only knows phases A and B. WS-12.4 shares no key with any A1 task and sits on the safety path (12.4 then 13.3, 13.4). | None: it cannot collide with A1 work. |
+| R-2 | No phase-B task is dispatched until every phase-A task of W1 is done. pick_ready --phase B lists 9 B tasks as FREE only because the tracker deps column omits phase-A dependencies. When B opens: WS-10.1 and WS-10.3 first, then WS-10.2 and WS-10.4 (bug round B-6, owner asked for it first), then 12.5, 13.4, 13.5, 13.7. | Plan section 5.3 phases; every suite imports the shared packages. | A B task would test against a half-edited platform file. |
+| R-3 | WS-04.6 is held until WS-06.8, WS-07.7 and WS-07.3 are done, although its Depends on lists only WS-04.5. | Its test and its Cross-workstream line need start_service_event_plane (06.8), install_rate_limiting (07.7) and ServiceTokenManager (07.3). Docs lane should add the three deps to the plan and tracker. | 04.6 would fail on ImportError or its implementer would stub the imports. |
+| R-4 | Security chain order: WS-07.3, then 07.2, then 07.4, then 07.5. WS-07.6 (keys: its own rate_limiting.py only) may run beside it; WS-07.7 shares the src/solace_security and tests/solace_security suite keys, so it waits for a gap. | 07.2, 07.3 and 07.5 share auth.py, 07.4 and 07.5 share middleware.py; 07.3 unblocks WS-10.4, 11.2, 13.5, 14.6 and 14.8. | One extra serial hop. |
+| R-5 | WS-05.21 waits for WS-03.19, WS-09.18, WS-09.4 and WS-07.7 and for W0 task WS-05.4. WS-05.8 waits for 05.21 and for W0 task WS-05.6. Then 05.9, then 05.22 (needs 12.3). None is in round 1. | The tracker shows WS-05.4 and WS-05.6 blocked; 05.21 consumes names and routes those tasks produce although its header lists only WS-05.4. | The dev stack would lose its fake-LLM wiring before the Bedrock path exists. |
+| R-6 | Schema lane is serial through the migrations key: WS-12.4 (slot 014), then WS-13.3 (slot 015, depends on 12.4); WS-14.4 (slot 012) in any free gap. Slot 010 was filled in W0 by WS-06.1 (done). | One linear Alembic chain; each real-infra test upgrades all slots. | Concurrent runs would corrupt each other's database. |
+| R-7 | tests/integration is shared by WS-07.3, WS-05.20 and later WS-10.x and WS-12.9. WS-05.20 runs after WS-09.3 and WS-07.3 are done, never beside another lane that edits tests/integration. | Key suite:tests/integration. | A regression run fails on another lane's half-edit. |
+| R-8 | WS-09.3's Step 5 lists exact failures that other tasks own (WS-05.20 and WS-10/12 edits). They are not regressions and are not fixed in 09.3. | The task text names them. | A fix round would edit another task's files. |
+| R-9 | WS-05.9 takes the Alertmanager values of WS-04 row 1693: SMARTHOST mailpit:1025, EMAIL_TO ops-alerts@test.invalid, EMAIL_FROM alerts@test.invalid, SMTP_REQUIRE_TLS false. Appended as an addendum to the WS-05.9 brief. | WS-05.9 says FROM ops-alertmanager@test.invalid and omits REQUIRE_TLS; WS-04.7's e2e accepts only ops-alerts@ and alerts@ addresses; mailpit has no TLS and WS-03.6 defaults REQUIRE_TLS true in strict. | Alert mails never reach mailpit in the strict profile, or WS-04.7's e2e fails on the address set. |
+| R-10 | WS-13.13 runs after WS-10.3 and WS-12.8; WS-10.4 carries bug round B-6 although the plan has no note. | 13.13 verifies every producer's contract and does not list those two. | A producer's contract mismatch found late. |
+| R-11 | WS-04.7's alert rules are proved with promtool rule tests on synthetic series; live firing is a wave-exit fault test, not a 04.7 gate. | The real series come from 06.10 and WS-13 tasks that land later. | None. |
+| R-12 | The plans and briefs still name checkpoint.ps1 and D:\tmp\mvp-exec\tools. Implementers use `python docs/mvp/exec-state/tools/mvp_exec.py checkpoint <label>` (cloud-addendum.md table). | Those scripts belong to the old Windows workspace. | None. |
+| R-13 | W1 dispatch (and any WS-05 task) waits until finalize records W0 exit. The tracker still shows WS-05.4, 05.5 and 05.6 as blocked. | W1 entry criterion 1. | Building on an unproven harness. |
